# befui for HTML and htmx

> Every befui component is a custom element in a plain HTML page: one CSS file and one script, no React in your app. Build them with `npm run build:html` (dist/html/befui.css and befui.js).

```html
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/befui.css">
<script type="module" src="/befui.js"></script>
<bef-button variant="soft">Save</bef-button>
```

## Rules
- Tag = `bef-` + kebab-case export name: Button -> <bef-button>, DataTable -> <bef-data-table>, DialogTrigger -> <bef-dialog-trigger>. Icons too: <bef-home-icon>.
- Props are attributes in kebab-case (default-value, as-child). A bare attribute is true. Strings are the default.
- JSON props use a colon: :value="40", :items='[{"id":"a","title":"T"}]'. Inside that JSON, a string that starts with a tag becomes content: {"icon":"<bef-home-icon></bef-home-icon>"}. A string like "~2026-12-31" becomes a Date.
- A child with slot="name" becomes the prop `name` (<bef-button slot="action">Undo</bef-button>). Other children are the component's children.
- Dates as plain attributes: ~to="2026-12-31T09:00", ~selected="now", ~at="now-14m" (also as "~now-14m" strings inside :json).
- Callbacks: emit="value-change open-change" fires bubbling events bef:value-change and bef:open-change (value in event.detail). The tag also writes the new value back to itself (:value, :open...), so controlled components work with no script; set :open="true" to open a controlled dialog.
- Props that are functions or JSX (render, columns, onSubmit, onApply) cannot be attributes: set them from a <script type="module">, element.props = { columns: [...], onSubmit: fn }. It also works on a tag inside another tag, and attribute changes on nested tags redraw them. A render function can return markup: befui.h('bef-badge', { variant: 'success' }, 'Active') or an HTML string.
- Content React draws outside the tag (dialogs, menus, sheets) is not inside it in the DOM: use befui.on(el, 'click', (e) => ...) for page-level listeners that go away with el.
- Always load befui.js as <script type="module">.
- style works on the outer tag. Do not invent Tailwind classes in HTML (they are not in the CSS); use inline style for layout.
- Works with htmx: elements added by a swap upgrade by themselves and hx-* attributes inside are processed. Radix inputs submit with the surrounding <form>.
- Dark mode: the dark class on <html>. Theme: the same CSS variables (--primary, ...).
- window.befui.toast.success('Saved') shows a toast when <bef-toaster> is on the page.

# Examples

## Accordion (accordion)
Collapsible sections with a smooth height animation. Single or multiple open.
```html
<bef-accordion class="w-full max-w-lg" :default-open='["a"]' :items='[{"id":"a","title":"How are leave balances calculated?","content":"Accrual runs on the first of every month, pro-rated for new joiners."},{"id":"b","title":"Can managers delegate approvals?","content":"Yes. Set a delegate for any date range from the approvals page."},{"id":"c","title":"Where is payroll data stored?","content":"Encrypted at rest, in the region you pick at signup."}]'></bef-accordion>
```

## Activity feed (activity-feed)
Who did what and when, grouped under Today and Yesterday, with live relative times and an optional quoted detail.
```html
<bef-activity-feed class="w-full max-w-md" :items='[{"id":"1","actor":"Aarav Mehta","action":"commented on","target":"Leave policy 2025","at":"~now-12m","icon":"<bef-message-square-icon></bef-message-square-icon>","detail":"“Carry-forward cap should be 12 days, not 10. Checked with Finance.”"},{"id":"2","actor":"Diya Rao","action":"uploaded","target":"offer-letter-v3.pdf","at":"~now-2h","icon":"<bef-file-icon></bef-file-icon>","detail":"248 KB · PDF"},{"id":"3","actor":"Isha Nair","action":"added","target":"Kabir Shah to Engineering","at":"~now-5h","icon":"<bef-user-plus-icon></bef-user-plus-icon>"},{"id":"4","actor":"Rohan Das","action":"merged","target":"Payroll export fixes","at":"~now-27h","icon":"<bef-git-merge-icon></bef-git-merge-icon>"},{"id":"5","actor":"Meera Iyer","action":"approved","target":"September expense report","at":"~now-30h"},{"id":"6","actor":"Vikram Joshi","action":"closed","target":"Duplicate employee IDs","at":1791628596217}]'></bef-activity-feed>
```

## Agent trace (agent-trace)
An AI agent run as a tree of model calls, tool calls and lookups, each with a bar on a shared time scale. Click a step for its input and output.
```html
<bef-agent-trace class="w-[48rem] max-w-full" :spans='[{"id":"run","name":"support_agent","kind":"agent","status":"done","start":0,"duration":5200,"children":[{"id":"plan","name":"claude-sonnet · plan","kind":"llm","status":"done","start":0,"duration":1100,"tokens":1820,"input":"Customer: \"Where is my order 4471?\"","output":"Plan: look up the order, then the courier status."},{"id":"order","name":"get_order","kind":"tool","status":"done","start":1100,"duration":380,"input":"{ \"id\": 4471 }","output":"{ \"status\": \"shipped\", \"carrier\": \"Delhivery\", \"awb\": \"DL88213\" }"},{"id":"kb","name":"search_help_center","kind":"retrieval","status":"done","start":1500,"duration":640,"input":"delivery delays","output":"3 articles"},{"id":"track","name":"track_shipment","kind":"tool","status":"error","start":1500,"duration":2300,"input":"{ \"awb\": \"DL88213\" }","output":"Error: courier API timed out after 2s"},{"id":"reply","name":"claude-sonnet · reply","kind":"llm","status":"done","start":3900,"duration":1300,"tokens":2310,"output":"Your order shipped with Delhivery (AWB DL88213). Live tracking is down right now, so I will email you once it is out for delivery."}]}]'></bef-agent-trace>
```

## AI chat (ai-chat)
A whole AI chat in one piece: a conversation that follows the newest message with a Latest button, a greeting and starter prompts while empty, a thinking row and the prompt box pinned below.
```html
<bef-ai-chat class="max-w-2xl" :messages="[]" value="" :thinking="false" :streaming="false" greeting="What are you working on?" :suggestions='[{"id":"1","label":"Summarise this week","hint":"Attendance, leave and approvals","icon":"<bef-calendar-icon></bef-calendar-icon>"},{"id":"2","label":"Draft an offer letter","hint":"From a role and a salary","icon":"<bef-file-text-icon></bef-file-text-icon>"},{"id":"3","label":"Who is out tomorrow?","hint":"Across all teams","icon":"<bef-users-icon></bef-users-icon>"}]' placeholder="Message the assistant" emit="value-change"></bef-ai-chat>
<script type="module">
  const chat = document.querySelector('bef-ai-chat');
  const replies = [
    'Here is the short version: 142 people are in, 9 are on leave, and 6 approvals are waiting for you.',
    'Sure. I would start with the role, the band and the joining date, then add the notice period and the probation terms.',
  ];
  let list = [];
  let n = 0;
  let timer;
  const put = () => chat.setAttribute(':messages', JSON.stringify(list));
  const send = (text) => {
    const reply = replies[n++ % replies.length];
    chat.setAttribute('value', '');
    list = [...list, { id: `u${Date.now()}`, role: 'user', content: text }];
    put();
    chat.setAttribute(':thinking', 'true');
    chat.setAttribute(':streaming', 'true');
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      chat.setAttribute(':thinking', 'false');
      list = [...list, { id: `a${Date.now()}`, role: 'assistant', plain: reply, content: `<bef-streaming-text text="${reply}" speed="20" :caret="false"></bef-streaming-text>` }];
      put();
      setTimeout(() => chat.setAttribute(':streaming', 'false'), (reply.split(' ').length / 20) * 1000 + 400);
    }, 1400);
  };
  chat.props = {
    onSubmit: send,
    onSuggestion: (s) => send(s.label),
    onStop: () => { clearTimeout(timer); chat.setAttribute(':thinking', 'false'); chat.setAttribute(':streaming', 'false'); },
  };
</script>
```

## AI message (ai-message)
One message in an AI chat: a right-aligned user bubble, or an assistant answer with copy, regenerate and thumbs actions and a blinking caret while it streams.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl gap-6">
  <bef-ai-message role="user">How many leave days can I carry forward?</bef-ai-message>
  <bef-ai-message role="assistant" plain="Carry-forward is capped at 12 days a year, and anything above that lapses on 31 March." id="g1">
    <p>Carry-forward is capped at <strong>12 days</strong> a year<bef-cite :n="1" href="#policy" title="Leave policy 2025"></bef-cite>, and anything above that lapses on 31 March<bef-cite :n="2" href="#faq" title="Leave FAQ"></bef-cite>.</p>
    <p>Casual leave does not carry forward at all.</p>
  </bef-ai-message>
  <bef-ai-message role="assistant" streaming>Checking the policy for the part about encashment</bef-ai-message>
</div>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onRegenerate: () => {
  },
};
</script>
<script type="module">
  const reply = document.querySelectorAll('bef-ai-message')[1];
  reply.props = { onFeedback: (v) => reply.setAttribute('feedback', v ?? '') };
</script>
```

## Alert dialog (alert-dialog)
Confirm before something destructive. Cancel and confirm, controlled by you.
```html
<bef-button variant="destructive"><bef-trash-icon></bef-trash-icon> Delete employee</bef-button>
<bef-alert-dialog :open="false" destructive title="Delete this employee?" description="Their records are removed from payroll and cannot be recovered." confirm-label="Delete" emit="open-change" id="g2"></bef-alert-dialog>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g2").props = {
  onConfirm: () => befui.toast.success("Employee deleted"),
};
</script>
<script type="module">
  const dialog = document.querySelector('bef-alert-dialog');
  document.querySelector('bef-button').addEventListener('click', () => dialog.setAttribute(':open', 'true'));
</script>
```

## Alert (alert)
Inline message in info, success, warning and danger.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-lg gap-3">
  <bef-alert variant="info" title="Payroll locks on the 25th">Submit attendance corrections before then.</bef-alert>
  <bef-alert variant="success" title="Leave approved"></bef-alert>
  <bef-alert variant="warning" title="3 documents expire this month">Review them in the compliance tab.</bef-alert>
  <bef-alert variant="danger" title="Sync failed">The biometric device did not respond. Retrying in 5 minutes.</bef-alert>
</div>
```

## Analog clock (analog-clock)
SVG clock for any time zone, with a second hand that ticks with a small bounce.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-8">
  <div class="grid justify-items-center gap-2">
    <bef-analog-clock time-zone="Asia/Kolkata" :size="128"></bef-analog-clock>
    <span class="text-sm font-medium">Mumbai</span>
  </div>
  <div class="grid justify-items-center gap-2">
    <bef-analog-clock time-zone="Europe/London" :size="128"></bef-analog-clock>
    <span class="text-sm font-medium">London</span>
  </div>
  <div class="grid justify-items-center gap-2">
    <bef-analog-clock time-zone="America/New_York" :size="128"></bef-analog-clock>
    <span class="text-sm font-medium">New York</span>
  </div>
</div>
```

## API key manager (api-key-manager)
List, create and revoke API keys. A new key shows once with Show and Copy; stored keys show only their start. Revoke asks first.
```html
<bef-api-key-manager id="keys" class="w-[40rem] max-w-full" :keys='[{"id":"1","name":"Production server","prefix":"sk_live_4f2a","created":"12 Aug","lastUsed":"2 min ago"},{"id":"2","name":"CI pipeline","prefix":"sk_live_9c1d","created":"3 Sep","lastUsed":"4 days ago"},{"id":"3","name":"Old staging","prefix":"sk_test_77be","created":"2 Jan","revoked":true}]'></bef-api-key-manager>
<script type="module">
  // onCreate and onRevoke are functions, so they are set as props and write the new list back to :keys
  const el = document.getElementById('keys');
  const keys = () => JSON.parse(el.getAttribute(':keys'));
  const save = (next) => el.setAttribute(':keys', JSON.stringify(next));
  el.props = {
    onCreate: (name) => save([{ id: String(keys().length + 1), name, prefix: 'sk_live_', secret: 'sk_live_b83f19a7c2e44d0f91ab', created: 'Today' }, ...keys()]),
    onRevoke: (id) => save(keys().map((k) => (k.id === id ? { ...k, revoked: true, secret: undefined } : k))),
  };
</script>
```

## App shell (app-shell)
Sidebar, topbar and scrolling content area, plus a page header.
```html
<div class="h-[420px] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border text-left">
  <bef-app-shell class="h-full" :sidebar='"<bef-sidebar><bef-sidebar-header><span class=\"text-sm font-semibold\">My app</span></bef-sidebar-header><bef-sidebar-content><bef-sidebar-item :icon=&#39;\"<bef-home-icon></bef-home-icon>\"&#39; label=\"Home\" active></bef-sidebar-item><bef-sidebar-item :icon=&#39;\"<bef-users-icon></bef-users-icon>\"&#39; label=\"Team\"></bef-sidebar-item><bef-sidebar-item :icon=&#39;\"<bef-settings-icon></bef-settings-icon>\"&#39; label=\"Settings\"></bef-sidebar-item></bef-sidebar-content></bef-sidebar>"'>
    <bef-topbar>
      <button class="ml-2 hidden h-8 w-56 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-muted/60 px-2.5 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground sm:flex"><bef-search-icon class="size-3.5"></bef-search-icon> Search <bef-kbd class="ml-auto">/</bef-kbd></button>
      <bef-button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" class="ml-auto" aria-label="Notifications">
        <bef-bell-icon></bef-bell-icon>
      </bef-button>
    </bef-topbar>
    <bef-app-content>
      <bef-page-header title="Home" description="Content scrolls here, the sidebar and topbar stay put." :actions='"<bef-button size=\"sm\">New</bef-button>"'></bef-page-header>
      <bef-card class="p-5 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Your page goes here.</bef-card>
    </bef-app-content>
  </bef-app-shell>
</div>
```

## Approval flow (approval-flow)
Chain of approvers with decisions, comments and times. The current approver gets Approve and Reject buttons.
```html
<div class="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border bg-card p-5">
  <p class="mb-4 text-sm font-semibold">Laptop purchase · ₹1,45,000</p>
  <bef-approval-flow :steps='[{"id":"1","approver":"Rohan Das","role":"Team lead","status":"approved","comment":"Looks fine, within the team budget.","at":"07 Oct"},{"id":"2","approver":"Meera Iyer","role":"Finance","status":"pending"},{"id":"3","approver":"Aarav Mehta","role":"CTO","status":"waiting"}]'></bef-approval-flow>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const flow = document.querySelector('bef-approval-flow');
  let steps = JSON.parse(flow.getAttribute(':steps'));
  flow.props = {
    onDecide(id, decision, comment) {
      steps = steps.map((s, i) => {
        if (s.id === id) return { ...s, status: decision, comment: comment || undefined, at: 'Just now' };
        if (decision === 'approved' && steps[i - 1]?.id === id) return { ...s, status: 'pending' };
        return s;
      });
      flow.setAttribute(':steps', JSON.stringify(steps));
      befui.toast[decision === 'approved' ? 'success' : 'info'](decision === 'approved' ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected');
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Area chart (area-chart)
Smooth gradient areas, stacked or overlapping, that wipe in on scroll. Hover for a readout across series.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl gap-4">
  <bef-segmented value="stacked" :options='[{"value":"stacked","label":"Stacked"},{"value":"overlap","label":"Overlap"}]' class="justify-self-start" emit="value-change"></bef-segmented>
  <bef-area-chart stacked :labels='["Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct"]' :series='[{"name":"Organic","data":[12,14,13,18,21,24,27]},{"name":"Paid","data":[6,8,9,10,12,11,14]},{"name":"Referral","data":[3,3,4,5,6,8,9]}]' id="g1"></bef-area-chart>
</div>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `${n}K`,
};
</script>
```

## Artifact panel (artifact-panel)
The side panel next to a chat for what the AI made: Preview and Code tabs, copy, download, expand and close.
```html
<bef-artifact-panel class="h-80" title="Greeting component" language="tsx" filename="greeting.tsx" code="export function Greeting({ name }: { name: string }) {
  return &lt;h1 className=&quot;text-2xl font-semibold&quot;&gt;Hello, {name}&lt;/h1&gt;;
}
">
  <h1 class="text-2xl font-semibold">Hello, Aarav</h1>
</bef-artifact-panel>
```

## Attachment (attachment)
A file attached to a prompt: icon or thumbnail, name, size, an upload progress line, an error state and a remove button.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  <bef-attachment name="offer-letter-v3.pdf" size="248 KB"></bef-attachment>
  <bef-attachment name="org-chart.png" size="1.2 MB"></bef-attachment>
  <bef-attachment name="payroll-october.xlsx" status="uploading" :progress="35"></bef-attachment>
  <bef-attachment name="scan-0042.png" status="error" id="g3"></bef-attachment>
</div>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g3").props = {
  onRemove: () => {
  },
};
</script>
<script type="module">
  document.querySelectorAll('bef-attachment[on-remove], bef-attachment').forEach((el) => {
    if (el.getAttribute('status')) return;
    el.props = { onRemove: () => el.remove() };
  });
</script>
```

## Audit log (audit-log)
Who changed what and when, with expandable before and after values.
```html
<bef-audit-log class="w-full max-w-xl" :entries='[{"id":"1","actor":"Meera Iyer","action":"updated","target":"Salary structure, Engineering","at":"~now-30m","ip":"10.0.4.21","changes":[{"field":"Basic","from":"₹60,000","to":"₹64,000"},{"field":"HRA","from":"₹24,000","to":"₹25,600"},{"field":"Effective from","from":"","to":"01 Nov 2026"}]},{"id":"2","actor":"Isha Nair","action":"created","target":"Employee EMP-0412","at":"~now-3h","ip":"10.0.4.18","changes":[{"field":"Name","to":"Ananya Kulkarni"},{"field":"Department","to":"Engineering"}]},{"id":"3","actor":"Kabir Shah","action":"exported","target":"Payroll report, September","at":"~now-20h","ip":"10.0.7.5"},{"id":"4","actor":"Rohan Das","action":"deleted","target":"Draft offer letter v1","at":"~now-30h","ip":"10.0.4.33"},{"id":"5","actor":"Aarav Mehta","action":"updated","target":"Leave policy 2026","at":"~now-2d","ip":"10.0.4.2","changes":[{"field":"Carry-forward cap","from":"10 days","to":"12 days"}]}]'></bef-audit-log>
```

## Autocomplete (autocomplete)
Text input that suggests as you type, from a fixed list or an async search (address, place). Keyboard friendly.
```html
<bef-autocomplete class="w-80" value="" placeholder="Search a place" emit="value-change" id="g1"></bef-autocomplete>
<script type="module">
const places = ['Bandra, Mumbai', 'Bangalore, Karnataka', 'Banjara Hills, Hyderabad', 'Baner, Pune', 'Bhopal, Madhya Pradesh', 'Connaught Place, Delhi', 'Koramangala, Bangalore', 'Salt Lake, Kolkata'];

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onSearch: (q) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r(places.filter((p) => p.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))), 300)),
};
</script>
```

## Autosize textarea (autosize-textarea)
A textarea that grows with the text up to a set number of lines, then scrolls, with an optional live character counter.
```html
<bef-field label="Message" for="msg" hint="Grows as you type, up to eight lines.">
  <bef-autosize-textarea id="msg" class="w-96 max-w-full" :min-rows="2" :max-rows="8" :max-length="280" placeholder="Write something..." default-value="Hi team,
The launch moved to Friday."></bef-autosize-textarea>
</bef-field>
```

## Avatar upload (avatar-upload)
Click-to-change profile picture with instant preview, size and type checks, and a Remove link.
```html
<bef-avatar-upload name="Aarav Mehta" emit="change"></bef-avatar-upload>
```

## Avatar (avatar)
Image with initials fallback in a stable color. Five sizes, circle or square, presence dot (online, away, busy, offline), a stacked group with hover names, and UserInfo for avatar plus name and subtitle.
```html
<div class="grid justify-items-start gap-6">
  <div class="flex items-end gap-3">
    <bef-avatar name="Aarav Mehta" size="xs"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Diya Rao" size="sm"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Kabir Shah" size="md"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Meera Iyer" size="lg"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Rohan Das" size="xl"></bef-avatar>
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
    <bef-avatar name="Aarav Mehta" size="md" status="online"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Diya Rao" size="md" status="away"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Kabir Shah" size="md" status="busy"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Meera Iyer" size="md" status="offline"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Acme Labs" size="md" shape="square"></bef-avatar>
    <bef-avatar name="Globex" size="md" shape="square" status="online"></bef-avatar>
  </div>
  <bef-avatar-group :names='["Aarav Mehta","Diya Rao","Kabir Shah","Meera Iyer","Rohan Das","Isha Nair","Vikram Joshi"]' :max="4"></bef-avatar-group>
  <bef-avatar-group :names='["Aarav Mehta","Diya Rao","Kabir Shah","Meera Iyer","Rohan Das","Isha Nair","Vikram Joshi"]' :max="3" size="md" shape="square"></bef-avatar-group>
  <div class="grid gap-3">
    <bef-user-info name="Aarav Mehta" subtitle="Senior engineer · Pune" status="online" size="md"></bef-user-info>
    <bef-user-info name="Diya Rao" subtitle="diya.rao@acme.in"></bef-user-info>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Badge (badge)
Status pill with semantic colors and an optional dot.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  <bef-badge>Default</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="outline">Outline</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="primary">Primary</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="success" dot>Approved</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="warning" dot>Pending</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="danger" dot>Rejected</bef-badge>
  <bef-badge variant="info"><bef-check-icon></bef-check-icon> Verified</bef-badge>
</div>
```

## Banner (banner)
Announcement bar that collapses smoothly when dismissed.
```html
<div class="w-full overflow-hidden rounded-lg border">
  <bef-banner persist-key="payroll-2" :action='"<a href=\"#/\" class=\"underline underline-offset-2\">Read more</a>"'>Payroll 2.0 is live for everyone.</bef-banner>
  <div class="p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Dismiss the bar and the page slides up. With persistKey it stays gone on your next visit (clear site data to see it again).</div>
</div>
```

## Bar list (bar-list)
Ranked list with a bar behind each row: top pages, products, sources, countries.
```html
<bef-bar-list class="w-full max-w-sm" :items='[{"label":"/pricing","value":12840},{"label":"/docs/getting-started","value":9210},{"label":"/blog/launch-notes","value":5632},{"label":"/signup","value":3120},{"label":"/changelog","value":1480}]'></bef-bar-list>
```

## Bar race (bar-race)
Bars that re-sort and grow as time moves, frame by frame, with play and a scrubber. Shows who overtakes whom.
```html
<bef-bar-race class="w-[36rem] max-w-full" :top="5" :frames='[{"label":"2021","values":{"Pune":210,"Mumbai":480,"Delhi":390,"Bengaluru":300,"Chennai":250,"Hyderabad":180}},{"label":"2022","values":{"Pune":260,"Mumbai":510,"Delhi":430,"Bengaluru":380,"Chennai":280,"Hyderabad":240}},{"label":"2023","values":{"Pune":320,"Mumbai":540,"Delhi":480,"Bengaluru":470,"Chennai":310,"Hyderabad":330}},{"label":"2024","values":{"Pune":410,"Mumbai":560,"Delhi":540,"Bengaluru":590,"Chennai":350,"Hyderabad":430}},{"label":"2025","values":{"Pune":520,"Mumbai":590,"Delhi":600,"Bengaluru":720,"Chennai":400,"Hyderabad":560}},{"label":"2026","values":{"Pune":640,"Mumbai":610,"Delhi":690,"Bengaluru":880,"Chennai":450,"Hyderabad":700}}]' id="g1"></bef-bar-race>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `${n}K`,
};
</script>
```

## Border beam (border-beam)
A light beam that circles the border. For pricing cards and featured content.
```html

  <div class="grid gap-3 p-6">
    <span class="text-xs font-medium text-primary">Most popular</span>

  </div>
</bef-border-beam>
```

## Bottom navigation (bottom-nav)
Mobile tab bar with icons, labels, an active pill and count badges.

</div>
```

## Bottom sheet (bottom-sheet)
Sheet that rises from the bottom, with a drag handle to dismiss. Radix Dialog underneath.
```html
<bef-button variant="outline">Apply for leave</bef-button>
<bef-bottom-sheet :open="false" title="Apply for" description="Drag the handle down to dismiss." emit="open-change">
  <ul class="grid gap-1 pb-2">
    <li>
      <button type="button" class="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium outline-none hover:bg-secondary focus-visible:bg-secondary">Casual leave</button>
    </li>
    <li>
      <button type="button" class="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium outline-none hover:bg-secondary focus-visible:bg-secondary">Sick leave</button>
    </li>
    <li>
      <button type="button" class="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium outline-none hover:bg-secondary focus-visible:bg-secondary">Earned leave</button>
    </li>
    <li>
      <button type="button" class="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium outline-none hover:bg-secondary focus-visible:bg-secondary">Work from home</button>
    </li>
  </ul>
</bef-bottom-sheet>
<script type="module">
  const sheet = document.querySelector('bef-bottom-sheet');
  document.querySelector('bef-button').addEventListener('click', () => sheet.setAttribute(':open', 'true'));
  // the options are drawn outside the tag (in the sheet), so listen on the page
  befui.on(sheet, 'click', (e) => {
    const option = e.target.closest('[role=dialog] li > button');
    if (!option) return;
    sheet.setAttribute(':open', 'false');
    befui.toast.success('Selected', option.textContent);
  });
</script>
```

## Box plot chart (box-plot-chart)
Spread per group: box for the middle half, median line, whiskers and outlier dots. Boxes open from the median; hover for the five numbers.

```

## Breadcrumb (breadcrumb)
Trail of parent pages.
```html
<bef-breadcrumb :items='[{"label":"People","href":"#"},{"label":"Employees","href":"#"},{"label":"Aarav Mehta"}]'></bef-breadcrumb>
```

## Bullet chart (bullet-chart)
A KPI against its target in one thin row, with shaded bands for poor, ok and good. The bar slides in on scroll.
```html
<bef-bullet-chart class="w-[34rem] max-w-full" :rows='[{"label":"Revenue (₹L)","value":72,"target":66,"ranges":[40,60,90]},{"label":"New customers","value":118,"target":150,"ranges":[80,120,180]},{"label":"Uptime (%)","value":99.94,"target":99.9,"ranges":[99,99.5,100]},{"label":"NPS","value":41,"target":50,"ranges":[20,40,70]}]'></bef-bullet-chart>
```

## Bump chart (bump-chart)
Rank over time as smooth lines that swap places. Lines draw in one by one; hover a line or name to follow it.
```html
<bef-bump-chart class="w-[38rem] max-w-full" :labels='["Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct"]' :series='[{"name":"Pune","ranks":[1,1,2,3,2]},{"name":"Mumbai","ranks":[2,3,1,1,1]},{"name":"Delhi","ranks":[3,2,3,2,3]},{"name":"Bengaluru","ranks":[4,4,4,4,4]}]'></bef-bump-chart>
```

## Butterfly chart (butterfly-chart)
Two bar sets back to back around shared rows, like a population pyramid. Bars grow outward from the middle.
```html
<bef-butterfly-chart class="w-[36rem] max-w-full" left-label="Mobile" right-label="Desktop" :rows='[{"label":"Home","left":5200,"right":3100},{"label":"Pricing","left":3400,"right":4200},{"label":"Docs","left":1800,"right":5600},{"label":"Blog","left":4100,"right":2300},{"label":"Signup","left":2600,"right":2900},{"label":"Settings","left":700,"right":1900}]'></bef-butterfly-chart>
```

## Button group (button-group)
Buttons joined into one row or column with shared borders and rounded outer corners.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-8">
  <bef-button-group aria-label="Range">
    <bef-button variant="outline">Day</bef-button>
    <bef-button variant="outline">Week</bef-button>
    <bef-button variant="outline">Month</bef-button>
  </bef-button-group>
  <bef-button-group orientation="vertical" aria-label="Zoom">
    <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Zoom in">
      <bef-plus-icon></bef-plus-icon>
    </bef-button>
    <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Zoom out">
      <bef-minus-icon></bef-minus-icon>
    </bef-button>
  </bef-button-group>
</div>
```

## Button (button)
Thirteen variants from plain to glow, shine and 3D press, pill shape, five sizes, loading state, and asChild for links.
```html
<bef-button><bef-download-icon></bef-download-icon> Export</bef-button>
```

## Calendar (calendar)
Month grid for one day or a range. Arrow keys, Page Up/Down, Home and End work.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-center gap-6">
  <div class="rounded-xl border bg-card p-3">
    <bef-calendar ~selected="now" emit="select"></bef-calendar>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border bg-card p-3">
    <bef-calendar mode="range" :week-starts-on="1" emit="select" id="g1"></bef-calendar>
  </div>
</div>
<script type="module">
const addDays = (d, n) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate() + n);
const startOfDay = (d) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());

const today = startOfDay(new Date());

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  disabled: (d) => d < addDays(today, -1),
};
</script>
```

A select with a column per level for nested choices like country, state, city. Keyboard friendly; gives the full path.

```

```

## Chat (chat)
Message bubbles with avatars, timestamps, a typing indicator and a composer. Enter sends.
```html
<bef-chat me="You" :messages='[{"id":"1","from":"Diya Rao","text":"Hey! Did the offer letter go out to Kabir?","at":"~now-14m"},{"id":"2","from":"Diya Rao","text":"He is asking about the joining date.","at":"~now-13m"},{"id":"3","from":"You","text":"Sent it this morning. Joining is 3 Nov.","at":"~now-9m"},{"id":"4","from":"Diya Rao","text":"Perfect, I will update the onboarding board.","at":"~now-8m"}]'></bef-chat>
<script type="module">
  const chat = document.querySelector('bef-chat');
  let messages = JSON.parse(chat.getAttribute(':messages'));
  const add = (from, text) => {
    messages = [...messages, { id: String(Date.now()), from, text, at: '~now' }];
    chat.setAttribute(':messages', JSON.stringify(messages));
  };
  chat.props = {
    onSend(text) {
      add('You', text);
      chat.setAttribute('typing', 'Diya Rao');
      setTimeout(() => { chat.removeAttribute('typing'); add('Diya Rao', 'Got it, thanks!'); }, 1500);
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Circle pack chart (circle-pack-chart)

## Citation popover (citation-popover)
A numbered citation inside an answer that opens a card with the site, title and quote on hover or focus. The number is a real link.
```html
<p class="max-w-lg text-sm leading-7">Unused casual leave lapses on 31 March<bef-citation-popover :n="1" :source='{"title":"Leave policy 2025: carry-forward and encashment","site":"handbook.acme.co","href":"#policy","quote":"Casual leave does not carry forward. Any balance at the end of the financial year lapses."}'></bef-citation-popover>, while up to 30 days of earned leave can be carried over<bef-citation-popover :n="2" :source='{"title":"Leave FAQ: what lapses on 31 March","site":"help.acme.co","href":"#faq","quote":"Earned leave carries forward up to a maximum of 30 days."}'></bef-citation-popover>.</p>
```

## Coachmark (coachmark)
A single new-feature hint pinned to one element with a Got it button. Lighter than a tour; can remember it was dismissed.

<div class="py-24">
  <bef-coachmark persist-key="demo-export" title="New: export to PDF" description="Send any report as a PDF from here.">
    <bef-button variant="outline">Export</bef-button>
  </bef-coachmark>
</div>
```

npx github:RandomKid24/befui add button card"></bef-code-block>
```

```

## Combo chart (combo-chart)
Bars, smooth lines and smooth areas on one chart, with an optional second scale on the right. Many values read together; hover a month for all of them.
```html
<bef-combo-chart class="w-[38rem] max-w-full" stacked :labels='["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep"]' :series='[{"name":"Revenue","type":"bar","data":[42,48,45,58,63,60,72,78,85]},{"name":"Costs","type":"bar","data":[30,33,35,38,41,44,46,49,52],"color":"text-destructive"},{"name":"Target","type":"area","data":[70,78,84,92,100,106,114,122,130],"color":"text-warning"},{"name":"Conversion","type":"line","axis":"right","data":[2.1,2.4,2.2,2.9,3.1,3,3.6,3.9,4.3],"color":"text-success"}]' id="g1"></bef-combo-chart>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `$${n}k`,
  formatRight: (n) => `${n}%`,
};
</script>
```

## Combobox (combobox)
Searchable select, single or multiple, with descriptions under each option.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xs gap-4">
  <bef-field label="Approver" for="appr">
    <bef-combobox id="appr" :options='[{"value":"aarav","label":"Aarav Mehta","description":"Engineering Manager"},{"value":"diya","label":"Diya Rao","description":"Product Designer"},{"value":"kabir","label":"Kabir Shah","description":"Account Executive"},{"value":"meera","label":"Meera Iyer","description":"HR Business Partner"},{"value":"rohan","label":"Rohan Das","description":"Backend Engineer"},{"value":"isha","label":"Isha Nair","description":"Marketing Lead"}]' placeholder="Choose a person" search-placeholder="Search people..." emit="value-change"></bef-combobox>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-field label="Notify" for="notify" hint="Pick as many as you like, or type a new name and choose Create.">
    <bef-multi-combobox id="notify" :options='[{"value":"aarav","label":"Aarav Mehta","description":"Engineering Manager"},{"value":"diya","label":"Diya Rao","description":"Product Designer"},{"value":"kabir","label":"Kabir Shah","description":"Account Executive"},{"value":"meera","label":"Meera Iyer","description":"HR Business Partner"},{"value":"rohan","label":"Rohan Das","description":"Backend Engineer"},{"value":"isha","label":"Isha Nair","description":"Marketing Lead"}]' :value='["diya"]' placeholder="Add people" emit="value-change"></bef-multi-combobox>
  </bef-field>
</div>
<script type="module">
  // onCreate is a function, so it is set as a prop: add the new option and select it
  const multi = document.getElementById('notify');
  multi.props = {
    onCreate: (label) => {
      const value = label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
      multi.setAttribute(':options', JSON.stringify([...JSON.parse(multi.getAttribute(':options')), { value, label }]));
      multi.setAttribute(':value', JSON.stringify([...JSON.parse(multi.getAttribute(':value') || '[]'), value]));
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Command palette (command)
Searchable palette on cmdk. Bind it to Ctrl or Cmd K.
```html
<bef-button variant="outline">Open palette <bef-kbd>⌘K</bef-kbd></bef-button>
<bef-command-dialog :open="false" emit="open-change">
  <bef-command-input placeholder="Type a command or search..."></bef-command-input>
  <bef-command-list>
    <bef-command-empty>No results.</bef-command-empty>
    <bef-command-group heading="People">
      <bef-command-item><bef-users-icon></bef-users-icon> Employee directory</bef-command-item>
      <bef-command-item><bef-calendar-icon></bef-calendar-icon> Leave calendar</bef-command-item>
    </bef-command-group>
    <bef-command-group heading="Reports">
      <bef-command-item><bef-file-icon></bef-file-icon> Payroll summary</bef-command-item>
    </bef-command-group>
  </bef-command-list>
</bef-command-dialog>
<script type="module">
  const palette = document.querySelector('bef-command-dialog');
  document.querySelector('bef-button').addEventListener('click', () => palette.setAttribute(':open', 'true'));
  // picking an item closes the palette
  befui.on(palette, 'click', (e) => e.target.closest('[cmdk-item]') && palette.setAttribute(':open', 'false'));
  befui.on(palette, 'keydown', (e) => e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.closest('[cmdk-root]') && palette.setAttribute(':open', 'false'));
</script>
```

## Comment thread (comment-thread)
Nested comments with inline reply boxes, three levels deep.
```html
<bef-comment-thread class="w-full max-w-lg" :comments='[{"id":"1","author":"Meera Shah","time":"2h ago","body":"Can we move the review to Thursday?","replies":[{"id":"2","author":"Rohan Iyer","time":"1h ago","body":"Thursday works for me."}]},{"id":"3","author":"Kabir Anand","time":"30m ago","body":"Added the updated budget sheet."}]'></bef-comment-thread>
<script type="module">
  const thread = document.querySelector('bef-comment-thread');
  let comments = JSON.parse(thread.getAttribute(':comments'));
  const add = (list, parent, c) => list.map((x) => (x.id === parent ? { ...x, replies: [...(x.replies ?? []), c] } : { ...x, replies: x.replies && add(x.replies, parent, c) }));
  thread.props = {
    onReply(parent, text) {
      comments = add(comments, parent, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), author: 'You', time: 'just now', body: text });
      thread.setAttribute(':comments', JSON.stringify(comments));
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Image compare (compare)
Before and after slider. Drag or use arrow keys.

<bef-compare class="w-[32rem] max-w-full" before="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%20640%20360%22%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%22640%22%20height%3D%22360%22%20fill%3D%22%231e293b%22%2F%3E%3Ctext%20x%3D%22320%22%20y%3D%22195%22%20font-family%3D%22sans-serif%22%20font-size%3D%2248%22%20font-weight%3D%22700%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20text-anchor%3D%22middle%22%3EBefore%3C%2Ftext%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E" after="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%20640%20360%22%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%22640%22%20height%3D%22360%22%20fill%3D%22%232563eb%22%2F%3E%3Ctext%20x%3D%22320%22%20y%3D%22195%22%20font-family%3D%22sans-serif%22%20font-size%3D%2248%22%20font-weight%3D%22700%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20text-anchor%3D%22middle%22%3EAfter%3C%2Ftext%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E"></bef-compare>
```

## Comparison table (comparison-table)
Plans across the top, features down the side, a check, dash or text in each cell. For pricing pages.
```html

```

## Coupon input (coupon-input)
Promo code field with async validation, an error message and a removable applied chip.
```html
<div class="w-full max-w-xs">
  <bef-coupon-input></bef-coupon-input>
  <p class="mt-2 px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Try WELCOME10 or FESTIVE250.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const coupon = document.querySelector('bef-coupon-input');
  const hint = document.querySelector('p');
  const codes = { WELCOME10: 'You save 10% on this order', FESTIVE250: 'You save ₹250' };
  coupon.props = {
    async onApply(code) {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
      if (!codes[code]) return false;
      coupon.setAttribute('applied', code);
      coupon.setAttribute('hint', codes[code]);
      hint.hidden = true;
      return true;
    },
    onRemove() { coupon.removeAttribute('applied'); coupon.removeAttribute('hint'); hint.hidden = false; },
  };
</script>
```

## Credit card (credit-card)
Payment card preview that flips in 3D to show the CVV. Formats as the user types, guesses Visa, Mastercard, AmEx or RuPay, and can mask the number.
```html
<div class="grid justify-items-center gap-5">
  <bef-credit-card number="4111 1111 1111 1111" name="Aarav Mehta" expiry="08/28" cvv="" :flipped="false" emit="flip"></bef-credit-card>
  <div class="grid w-80 max-w-full grid-cols-2 gap-2">
    <bef-input class="col-span-2" aria-label="Card number" input-mode="numeric" :max-length="19" value="4111 1111 1111 1111" emit="change"></bef-input>
    <bef-input class="col-span-2" aria-label="Name" value="Aarav Mehta" emit="change"></bef-input>
    <bef-input aria-label="Expiry" placeholder="MM/YY" :max-length="5" value="08/28" emit="change"></bef-input>
    <bef-input aria-label="CVV" placeholder="CVV" input-mode="numeric" :max-length="4" value="" emit="change"></bef-input>
  </div>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const card = document.querySelector('bef-credit-card');
  const inputs = [...document.querySelectorAll('bef-input')];
  ['number', 'name', 'expiry', 'cvv'].forEach((field, i) => {
    inputs[i].addEventListener('bef:change', (e) => card.setAttribute(field, e.detail));
    // focusing the CVV flips the card over, any other field turns it back
    inputs[i].addEventListener('focusin', () => card.setAttribute(':flipped', String(field === 'cvv')));
  });
</script>
```

```

## Date picker (date-picker)
Date and date range fields that open a calendar. The range picker has optional quick presets (Last 7 days, This month...) and a day count. Closes on pick.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-4">
  <bef-field label="Joining date" for="join" hint="Clear it with the x.">
    <bef-date-picker id="join" clearable emit="change"></bef-date-picker>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-field label="Leave dates" for="leave" hint="Past dates are disabled.">
    <bef-date-range-picker id="leave" clearable emit="change"></bef-date-range-picker>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-field label="Report period" for="period" hint="Quick ranges on the left.">
    <bef-date-range-picker id="period" presets clearable emit="change"></bef-date-range-picker>
  </bef-field>
</div>
<script type="module">
const addDays = (d, n) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate() + n);
const startOfDay = (d) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());

const today = startOfDay(new Date());

document.getElementById("leave").props = {
  disabledDays: (d) => d < addDays(today, 0),
};
</script>
```

## Drawer (drawer)
Panel that slides in from any edge and follows your finger: drag the grip to dismiss. Bottom drawers can rest half open (snap points) and be pulled to full height.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2" id="drawer-buttons">
  <bef-button variant="outline" data-side="bottom" class="capitalize">Bottom</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" data-side="right" class="capitalize">Right</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" data-side="left" class="capitalize">Left</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" data-side="top" class="capitalize">Top</bef-button>
</div>
<bef-drawer :open="false" side="bottom" :snap-points="[0.5,1]" title="Order #4821" description="Drag the grip toward the edge to dismiss." emit="open-change">
  <ul class="grid gap-2 pb-4 text-sm">
    <li class="flex justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5"><span>Wireless keyboard</span><span class="text-muted-foreground">1 x</span></li>
    <li class="flex justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5"><span>USB-C hub</span><span class="text-muted-foreground">1 x</span></li>
    <li class="flex justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5"><span>Desk mat</span><span class="text-muted-foreground">1 x</span></li>
    <li class="flex justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5"><span>Monitor arm</span><span class="text-muted-foreground">1 x</span></li>
    <li class="flex justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5"><span>Laptop stand</span><span class="text-muted-foreground">1 x</span></li>
  </ul>
</bef-drawer>
<script type="module">
  const drawer = document.querySelector('bef-drawer');
  // set the side first and keep it after closing, so the drawer leaves through the edge it came from
  document.querySelectorAll('#drawer-buttons [data-side]').forEach((b) => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const side = b.dataset.side;
    drawer.setAttribute('side', side);
    drawer.setAttribute(':snap-points', side === 'bottom' ? '[0.5,1]' : '[1]'); // snap points are for the bottom drawer
    drawer.setAttribute(':open', 'true');
  }));
</script>
```

## Dumbbell chart (dumbbell-chart)
Change per item as two dots joined by a line: before and after, plan and actual. Green when good, red when bad.

## File manager (file-manager)
Folder browser with breadcrumb, list and grid views, selection and double-click or Enter to open.
```html
<bef-file-manager class="max-w-xl" :path='["Files"]' :items='[{"id":"hr","name":"HR","type":"folder","modified":"07 Oct"},{"id":"fin","name":"Finance","type":"folder","modified":"05 Oct"},{"id":"brand","name":"Brand assets","type":"folder","modified":"28 Sep"},{"id":"readme","name":"Welcome.pdf","type":"file","size":482000,"modified":"01 Sep"}]'></bef-file-manager>
<script type="module">
  const tree = {
    '': [{ id: 'hr', name: 'HR', type: 'folder', modified: '07 Oct' }, { id: 'fin', name: 'Finance', type: 'folder', modified: '05 Oct' }, { id: 'brand', name: 'Brand assets', type: 'folder', modified: '28 Sep' }, { id: 'readme', name: 'Welcome.pdf', type: 'file', size: 482000, modified: '01 Sep' }],
    hr: [{ id: 'policy', name: 'Leave policy 2026.pdf', type: 'file', size: 910000, modified: '06 Oct' }, { id: 'offer', name: 'Offer letter template.docx', type: 'file', size: 64000, modified: '02 Oct' }, { id: 'org', name: 'Org chart.png', type: 'image', size: 1840000, modified: '30 Sep' }],
    fin: [{ id: 'p', name: 'Payroll September.xlsx', type: 'file', size: 233000, modified: '05 Oct' }, { id: 'inv', name: 'Invoices', type: 'folder', modified: '03 Oct' }],
    brand: [{ id: 'logo', name: 'Logo.png', type: 'image', size: 54000, modified: '12 Sep' }, { id: 'guide', name: 'Brand guide.pdf', type: 'file', size: 5200000, modified: '12 Sep' }],
    inv: [{ id: 'i1', name: 'INV-204.pdf', type: 'file', size: 88000, modified: '03 Oct' }],
  };
  const files = document.querySelector('bef-file-manager');
  let trail = [];
  const draw = () => {
    files.setAttribute(':path', JSON.stringify(['Files', ...trail.map((t) => t.name)]));
    files.setAttribute(':items', JSON.stringify(tree[trail.length ? trail[trail.length - 1].id : ''] ?? []));
  };
  files.props = {
    onNavigate(depth) { trail = trail.slice(0, depth); draw(); },
    onOpen(item) { if (item.type === 'folder') { trail = [...trail, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]; draw(); } },
  };
</script>
```

## File tree (file-tree)
Folder and file tree like an editor sidebar: folders first, file-type icons, open and close, arrow-key navigation, full path on select.

<div class="grid w-72 max-w-full gap-3">
  <bef-file-tree class="rounded-xl border bg-card p-2" :nodes='[{"name":"src","children":[{"name":"app","children":[{"name":"page.tsx"},{"name":"layout.tsx"},{"name":"globals.css"}]},{"name":"components","children":[{"name":"button.tsx"},{"name":"card.tsx"},{"name":"ui","children":[]}]},{"name":"lib","children":[{"name":"utils.ts"}]}]},{"name":"public","children":[{"name":"logo.svg"},{"name":"hero.png"}]},{"name":"package.json"},{"name":"README.md"},{"name":"tsconfig.json"}]' selected="src/app/page.tsx" :default-open='["src","src/app"]' emit="select"></bef-file-tree>
  <p class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Opened: <code>src/app/page.tsx</code></p>
</div>
```

## File uploader (file-uploader)
Multi-file drop area with a progress bar per file, cancel, retry and a concurrency limit. You supply the upload function.
```html
<bef-file-uploader class="w-full max-w-md" id="g1"></bef-file-uploader>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  upload: (file, onProgress, signal) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  let p = 0;
  const id = setInterval(() => {
    p += 8 + Math.random() * 12;
    if (file.name.toLowerCase().includes("fail") && p > 55) {
      clearInterval(id);
      return reject(new Error("Server rejected the file."));
    };
    onProgress(Math.min(p, 100));
    if (p >= 100) {
      clearInterval(id);
      resolve();
    }
  }, 250);
  signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
    clearInterval(id);
    reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
  });
}),
};
</script>
```

## Filter bar (filter-bar)
Removable filter chips with an Add filter menu: pick a field, then a value.

<bef-filter-bar :value='{"status":"Active"}' :fields='[{"key":"status","label":"Status","options":["Active","On leave","Exited"]},{"key":"dept","label":"Department","options":["Engineering","Sales","People","Finance"]},{"key":"type","label":"Type","options":["Full-time","Contract","Intern"]}]' emit="change"></bef-filter-bar>
```

## Filter builder (filter-builder)
Field, operator and value rows that all must match, like the Notion or Linear filter, with a helper to filter an array.
```html
<div class="grid w-[40rem] max-w-full gap-4">
  <bef-filter-builder id="filters" :rules='[{"field":"team","op":"is","value":"Engineering"}]' :fields='[{"key":"name","label":"Name"},{"key":"team","label":"Team","type":"select","options":["Engineering","Design","Sales","People"]},{"key":"age","label":"Age","type":"number"}]' emit="change"></bef-filter-builder>
  <p id="filter-result" class="text-sm text-muted-foreground"></p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const people = [
    { name: 'Aarav Mehta', team: 'Engineering', age: 31 }, { name: 'Diya Rao', team: 'Design', age: 27 }, { name: 'Kabir Shah', team: 'Sales', age: 35 },
    { name: 'Meera Iyer', team: 'People', age: 29 }, { name: 'Rohan Das', team: 'Engineering', age: 41 }, { name: 'Isha Nair', team: 'Sales', age: 26 },
  ];
  const builder = document.getElementById('filters');
  // the same matching rules the React matchesFilters helper uses
  const pass = (row, r) => {
    if (r.value === '') return true;
    const raw = row[r.field], v = String(raw ?? '').toLowerCase(), q = r.value.toLowerCase();
    if (r.op === 'contains') return v.includes(q);
    if (r.op === 'is') return v === q;
    if (r.op === 'is not') return v !== q;
    if (r.op === 'starts with') return v.startsWith(q);
    if (r.op === '=') return Number(raw) === Number(r.value);
    if (r.op === '>') return Number(raw) > Number(r.value);
    if (r.op === '<') return Number(raw) < Number(r.value);
    if (r.op === 'between') { const [a, b] = r.value.split('-').map(Number); return Number(raw) >= (a ?? -Infinity) && Number(raw) <= (b ?? Infinity); }
    return true;
  };
  const show = (rules) => {
    const rows = people.filter((p) => rules.every((r) => pass(p, r)));
    document.getElementById('filter-result').textContent = `${rows.length} of ${people.length}: ${rows.map((r) => r.name).join(', ') || 'no matches'}`;
  };
  show(JSON.parse(builder.getAttribute(':rules')));
  builder.addEventListener('bef:change', (e) => {
    builder.setAttribute(':rules', JSON.stringify(e.detail)); // write the new rules back so the tag keeps them
    show(e.detail);
  });
</script>
```

## Forecast chart (forecast-chart)
Solid line for what happened, dashed line inside a shaded band for what is expected. The band is the likely range.

</script>
```

## Form wizard (form-wizard)
A form split into steps: stepper on top, one step at a time, Back and Next with per-step validation.
```html
<bef-form-wizard id="wizard" class="w-[32rem] max-w-full" finish-label="Create account"></bef-form-wizard>
<script type="module">
  // each step's content is plain HTML; validate returns true to move on, or a message to show instead
  const field = (name) => document.querySelector(`#wizard input[name=${name}]`)?.value ?? '';
  const data = { name: '', email: '' };
  document.getElementById('wizard').props = {
    onFinish: () => befui.toast.success('Account created', data.name),
    steps: [
      { title: 'You', description: 'Who are you', content: '<bef-input name="name" aria-label="Name" placeholder="Full name"></bef-input>',
        validate: () => ((data.name = field('name').trim()) ? true : 'Enter your name to continue.') },
      { title: 'Contact', description: 'How to reach you', content: '<bef-input name="email" type="email" aria-label="Email" placeholder="you@company.com"></bef-input>',
        validate: () => ((data.email = field('email')), true) },
      { title: 'Review', content: '<p class="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Ready to create the account.</p>' },
    ],
  };
</script>
```

<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `₹${Math.round(n)}`,
};
</script>
```

## Hover card (hover-card)
Rich preview that opens when you hover or focus a link, for profiles and references.
```html
<p class="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Design review notes from <bef-hover-card>
  <bef-hover-card-trigger as-child>
    <a href="#ada" class="font-medium text-foreground underline underline-offset-4">@ada</a>
  </bef-hover-card-trigger>
  <bef-hover-card-content>
    <div class="flex gap-3">
      <bef-avatar name="Ada Lovelace" size="md"></bef-avatar>
      <div class="space-y-1">
        <p class="text-sm font-semibold">Ada Lovelace</p>
        <p class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Leads the design system. Reviews every new component before it ships.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </bef-hover-card-content>
</bef-hover-card> and the team.</p>
```

## Image gallery (image-gallery)
Masonry grid of images of different heights with a hover zoom and caption. onSelect gives the index.
```html
<bef-image-gallery class="w-[32rem] max-w-full" ## Image upload (image-upload)
Thumbnail grid with remove buttons, a cover label and a drop tile. Limits count, type and size.
```html
<bef-image-upload class="w-full max-w-md" :files="[]" :max="6" emit="files-change"></bef-image-upload>
```

## Inline edit (inline-edit)
Text that becomes a field on click. Enter saves, Escape cancels.
```html
<div class="w-64"><bef-inline-edit value="Q4 hiring plan" emit="save"></bef-inline-edit></div>
```

## Input group (input-group)
One field with text, icons or buttons attached at the start or end, like https:// or .com.
```html
<div class="flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col gap-4">
  <bef-input-group>
    <bef-input-addon>https://</bef-input-addon>
    <bef-input-group-input aria-label="Site name" placeholder="befui"></bef-input-group-input>
    <bef-input-addon side="end">.com</bef-input-addon>
  </bef-input-group>
  <bef-input-group>
    <bef-input-addon>$</bef-input-addon>
    <bef-input-group-input aria-label="Amount" type="number" placeholder="0.00"></bef-input-group-input>
    <bef-input-addon side="end">USD</bef-input-addon>
  </bef-input-group>
  <bef-input-group>
    <bef-input-addon class="border-e-0 bg-transparent pe-0">
      <bef-search-icon></bef-search-icon>
    </bef-input-addon>
    <bef-input-group-input aria-label="Search" placeholder="Search components"></bef-input-group-input>
    <bef-input-addon side="end" class="border-s-0 bg-transparent px-1.5">
      <bef-button size="xs">Go</bef-button>
    </bef-input-addon>
  </bef-input-group>
</div>
```

## Input (input)
Text input and textarea with icon, trailing slot, and invalid state.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-4">
  <bef-field label="Full name" for="name" required hint="As it appears on the offer letter.">
    <bef-input id="name" placeholder="Aarav Mehta"></bef-input>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-field label="Work email" for="email">
    <bef-input id="email" type="email" :left-icon='"<bef-mail-icon></bef-mail-icon>"' placeholder="you@company.com"></bef-input>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-field label="Employee ID" for="eid" error="This ID is already taken.">
    <bef-input id="eid" default-value="EMP-0042" aria-invalid></bef-input>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-input :left-icon='"<bef-search-icon></bef-search-icon>"' :right-slot='"<bef-kbd>/</bef-kbd>"' placeholder="Search..."></bef-input>
  <bef-field label="Notes" for="notes">
    <bef-textarea id="notes" placeholder="Anything the approver should know"></bef-textarea>
  </bef-field>
  <bef-input disabled placeholder="Disabled"></bef-input>
</div>
```

## Invoice (invoice)
Printable invoice with number, status, from and bill-to, line items, tax and total computed from the lines.
```html
<bef-invoice class="w-full max-w-2xl" number="INV-2026-0142" status="unpaid" issued="1 Oct 2026" due="15 Oct 2026" :tax-rate="0.18" :from='{"name":"Acme Industries Pvt Ltd","lines":["12 MG Road, Pune 411001","GSTIN 27AAACA1234A1Z9"]}' :to='{"name":"Northwind Traders","lines":["44 Park Street, Kolkata 700016","accounts@northwind.in"]}' :lines='[{"description":"Business plan, 12 seats, October","qty":12,"price":1299},{"description":"Extra storage (100 GB)","qty":2,"price":500}]' notes="Pay by bank transfer within 14 days. Late payments add 1.5% a month." :actions='"<bef-button size=\"sm\" variant=\"outline\"><bef-download-icon></bef-download-icon> PDF</bef-button>"'></bef-invoice>
```

## Kanban board (kanban)
Columns of draggable cards. Drag with the mouse or move a focused card with Alt and the arrow keys. Controlled, no drag library.
```html
<bef-kanban :columns='[{"id":"todo","title":"To do","tone":"default"},{"id":"doing","title":"In progress","tone":"info"},{"id":"review","title":"In review","tone":"warning"},{"id":"done","title":"Done","tone":"success"}]' :value='{"todo":[{"id":"t1","title":"Onboarding checklist for new hires","tag":"Feature","owner":"Isha Nair","due":"14 Oct"},{"id":"t2","title":"Fix payslip PDF margins","tag":"Bug","owner":"Rohan Das","due":"12 Oct"},{"id":"t3","title":"Archive 2023 attendance logs","tag":"Chore","owner":"Meera Iyer","due":"30 Oct"}],"doing":[{"id":"t4","title":"Leave balance widget","tag":"Feature","owner":"Aarav Mehta","due":"16 Oct"},{"id":"t5","title":"Refresh login screen","tag":"Design","owner":"Diya Rao","due":"15 Oct"}],"review":[{"id":"t6","title":"Holiday calendar import","tag":"Feature","owner":"Kabir Shah","due":"11 Oct"}],"done":[{"id":"t7","title":"Dark mode for dashboards","tag":"Design","owner":"Diya Rao","due":"03 Oct"},{"id":"t8","title":"Duplicate employee IDs","tag":"Bug","owner":"Vikram Joshi","due":"05 Oct"}]}' emit="change" id="g1"></bef-kanban>
<script type="module">
const { h: __h, T, Fragment: __Fragment } = befui;
const Avatar = T.Avatar;
const Badge = T.Badge;

const tagTone = { Bug: 'danger', Feature: 'info', Chore: 'default', Design: 'warning' };

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  renderCard: (t) => __h("div", { className: "grid gap-2" }, __h("p", { className: "font-medium leading-snug" }, t.title), __h("div", { className: "flex items-center justify-between gap-2" }, __h(T.Badge, { variant: tagTone[t.tag] }, t.tag), __h("span", { className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground" }, t.due, __h(T.Avatar, { name: t.owner, size: "xs" })))),
};
</script>
```

## Kbd (kbd)
Keyboard key hint.
```html
<p class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Press <bef-kbd>Ctrl</bef-kbd> <bef-kbd>K</bef-kbd> to search</p>
```

## KPI strip (kpi-strip)
A row of headline numbers sharing one border with dividers between them, with change arrows. The strip at the top of a dashboard.
```html
<bef-kpi-strip class="w-[44rem] max-w-full" :items='[{"label":"Revenue","value":"₹48.2L","delta":12.5,"hint":"vs last month"},{"label":"Active users","value":"8,420","delta":4.1},{"label":"Churn","value":"2.3%","delta":-0.6,"inverse":true},{"label":"Open tickets","value":"64","delta":9,"inverse":true}]'></bef-kpi-strip>
```

## Label and Field (label)
Label with required marker, plus Field for label, control and hint or error.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-5">
  <div class="grid gap-1.5">
    <bef-label for="a" required>Label on its own</bef-label>
    <bef-input id="a"></bef-input>
  </div>
  <bef-field label="Field wraps label, control and message" for="b" hint="Shown under the control.">
    <bef-input id="b"></bef-input>
  </bef-field>
</div>
```

## Live chart (live-chart)
Streaming line chart that slides left as new values arrive. You give it a function for the next value; pause it any time.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-lg gap-3">
  <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
    <span class="text-sm font-medium">Active users</span>
    <bef-button size="xs" variant="outline">Pause</bef-button>
  </div>
  <bef-live-chart :paused="false" id="g2"></bef-live-chart>
</div>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g2").props = {
  next: (p) => Math.max(10, Math.min(95, p + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14)),
};
</script>
<script type="module">
  const chart = document.querySelector('bef-live-chart');
  const button = document.querySelector('bef-button');
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const paused = chart.getAttribute(':paused') !== 'true';
    chart.setAttribute(':paused', String(paused));
    button.textContent = paused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
  });
</script>
```

    </div>
    <div class="grid justify-items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border py-4">

      <span class="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">bars</span>

      <span class="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">orbit</span>

      <bef-loader variant="dual"></bef-loader>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="grid gap-3">
    <bef-bar-loader></bef-bar-loader>
    <bef-bar-loader :value="64"></bef-bar-loader>
  </div>
  <bef-button size="sm" variant="outline" class="justify-self-start">Stop loading</bef-button>
  <bef-loading-overlay loading label="Fetching report" variant="orbit">
    <bef-card>
      <bef-card-header><bef-card-title>Monthly revenue</bef-card-title></bef-card-header>

      </bef-card-content>
    </bef-card>
  </bef-loading-overlay>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const overlay = document.querySelector('bef-loading-overlay');
  const button = [...document.querySelectorAll('bef-button')].pop();
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const loading = overlay.getAttribute(':loading') !== 'true';
    overlay.setAttribute(':loading', String(loading));
    button.textContent = loading ? 'Stop loading' : 'Reload report';
  });
</script>
```

## Onboarding checklist (onboarding-checklist)
Getting-started card with a progress bar, tick-off items, short help text and actions. Collapsible.
```html
<bef-onboarding-checklist :items='[{"id":"profile","title":"Complete your profile","done":true},{"id":"team","title":"Invite your team","description":"Add colleagues by email so they can see shared reports.","done":false,"action":"<bef-button size=\"xs\">Invite people</bef-button>"},{"id":"data","title":"Import employees","description":"Upload a CSV or connect your payroll tool.","done":false,"action":"<bef-button size=\"xs\" variant=\"outline\">Upload CSV</bef-button>"},{"id":"leave","title":"Set up leave policies","done":false}]'></bef-onboarding-checklist>
<script type="module">
  const list = document.querySelector('bef-onboarding-checklist');
  let items = JSON.parse(list.getAttribute(':items'));
  list.props = {
    onToggle(id, done) {
      items = items.map((i) => (i.id === id ? { ...i, done } : i));
      list.setAttribute(':items', JSON.stringify(items));
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Order summary (order-summary)
Cart lines with subtotal, discount, shipping, tax and total computed for you. Slots for a coupon field and a checkout button.
```html
<bef-order-summary :lines='[{"id":"1","name":"Wireless keyboard","detail":"Graphite · UK layout","qty":1,"price":3499},{"id":"2","name":"Noise-cancelling headphones","detail":"Midnight blue","qty":2,"price":8999},{"id":"3","name":"Standing desk mat","qty":1,"price":1299}]' :discount="0" :shipping="0" :tax-rate="0.18" :action='"<bef-button class=\"w-full\" size=\"lg\">Place order</bef-button>"'>
  <bef-coupon-input></bef-coupon-input>
</bef-order-summary>
<script type="module">
  const summary = document.querySelector('bef-order-summary');
  const coupon = document.querySelector('bef-coupon-input'); // a tag inside another tag stays live: its attributes and props can change later
  coupon.props = {
    onApply(code) {
      const ok = code === 'FESTIVE250';
      if (ok) { coupon.setAttribute('applied', code); coupon.setAttribute('hint', 'You save ₹250'); summary.setAttribute(':discount', '250'); }
      return ok;
    },
    onRemove() { coupon.removeAttribute('applied'); coupon.removeAttribute('hint'); summary.setAttribute(':discount', '0'); },
  };
</script>
```

## OTP input (otp-input)
Code boxes with paste, backspace, arrow keys, autofill and a shake on error.
```html
<div class="grid justify-items-center gap-3">
  <bef-otp-input value="" :invalid="false" emit="change"></bef-otp-input>
  <p class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Try 123456, or paste any code.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const otp = document.querySelector('bef-otp-input');
  otp.addEventListener('bef:change', () => otp.setAttribute(':invalid', 'false'));
  otp.props = {
    onComplete(code) {
      if (code === '123456') return befui.toast.success('Verified');
      otp.setAttribute(':invalid', 'true');
      setTimeout(() => otp.setAttribute('value', ''), 500);
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Pace chart (pace-chart)

<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `₹${n.toFixed(2)} Cr`,

<bef-parallel-coordinates class="w-[42rem] max-w-full" :groups='[{"name":"Petrol"},{"name":"Diesel SUV"},{"name":"Electric"}]' :rows='[{"name":"Sedan A","group":0,"values":{"mpg":28,"top":210,"acc":6.1,"price":14.2}},{"name":"Sedan B","group":0,"values":{"mpg":31,"top":190,"acc":7.4,"price":13.1}},{"name":"Hatch C","group":0,"values":{"mpg":38,"top":160,"acc":9.8,"price":11.4}},{"name":"Hatch D","group":0,"values":{"mpg":36,"top":170,"acc":9.1,"price":11.9}},{"name":"SUV E","group":1,"values":{"mpg":22,"top":230,"acc":8.2,"price":21.5}},{"name":"SUV F","group":1,"values":{"mpg":24,"top":215,"acc":8.9,"price":19.8}},{"name":"SUV G","group":1,"values":{"mpg":20,"top":250,"acc":7.1,"price":26}},{"name":"EV H","group":2,"values":{"mpg":0,"top":260,"acc":4.6,"price":38}},{"name":"EV I","group":2,"values":{"mpg":0,"top":220,"acc":6.2,"price":31.5}},{"name":"EV J","group":2,"values":{"mpg":0,"top":190,"acc":7.8,"price":24.9}}]' id="g1"></bef-parallel-coordinates>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  axes: [
    { key: "mpg", label: "Mileage", format: (n) => `${n} km/l` },
    { key: "top", label: "Top speed", format: (n) => `${n} km/h` },
    { key: "acc", label: "0-100 s", format: (n) => `${n}s` },
    { key: "price", label: "Price", format: (n) => `₹${n}L` },
  ],
};
</script>

## Prompt input (prompt-input)
The box you type a prompt into: grows with the text, Enter sends, Shift+Enter adds a line, and the send button turns into Stop while a reply streams.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl gap-3">
  <bef-prompt-input value="" :streaming="false" :max-length="400" placeholder="Ask about leave policy, payroll or onboarding" :leading='"<bef-button variant=\"ghost\" size=\"icon-sm\" aria-label=\"Attach a file\"><bef-paperclip-icon></bef-paperclip-icon></bef-button>"' emit="value-change"></bef-prompt-input>
  <p class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Enter sends, Shift+Enter adds a line. After sending it shows Stop for three seconds.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const box = document.querySelector('bef-prompt-input');
  let timer;
  const settle = () => { clearTimeout(timer); box.setAttribute(':streaming', 'false'); };
  box.props = {
    onSubmit() {
      box.setAttribute('value', '');
      box.setAttribute(':streaming', 'true');
      timer = setTimeout(settle, 3000);
    },
    onStop: settle,
  };
</script>
```

## Prompt suggestions (prompt-suggestions)
Starter prompts for an empty chat, as two-line cards or compact chips. Selecting one fills the prompt box.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl gap-5">
  <bef-prompt-suggestions :items='[{"id":"1","label":"Summarise this week","hint":"Attendance, leave and approvals","icon":"<bef-calendar-icon></bef-calendar-icon>"},{"id":"2","label":"Draft an offer letter","hint":"From a role and a salary","icon":"<bef-file-text-icon></bef-file-text-icon>"},{"id":"3","label":"Who is out tomorrow?","hint":"Across all teams","icon":"<bef-users-icon></bef-users-icon>"},{"id":"4","label":"Explain this payslip","hint":"Line by line, in plain words","icon":"<bef-sparkle-icon></bef-sparkle-icon>"}]'></bef-prompt-suggestions>
  <bef-prompt-suggestions layout="chips" :items='[{"id":"1","label":"Summarise this week","hint":"Attendance, leave and approvals"},{"id":"2","label":"Draft an offer letter","hint":"From a role and a salary"},{"id":"3","label":"Who is out tomorrow?","hint":"Across all teams"},{"id":"4","label":"Explain this payslip","hint":"Line by line, in plain words"}]'></bef-prompt-suggestions>
  <p class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Pick one.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const note = document.querySelector('p');
  document.querySelectorAll('bef-prompt-suggestions').forEach((el) => {
    el.props = { onSelect: (s) => { note.textContent = `Picked: ${s.label}`; } };
  });
</script>
```

## Pull to refresh (pull-to-refresh)
Scroll area you pull down from the top to refresh, with a spinner until your promise settles.
```html
<div class="w-full max-w-xs">
  <bef-pull-to-refresh class="h-64 rounded-xl border bg-card">
    <ul class="divide-y">
      <li class="px-4 py-3 text-[13px]">Aarav punched in</li>
      <li class="px-4 py-3 text-[13px]">Diya applied for leave</li>
      <li class="px-4 py-3 text-[13px]">Payroll draft created</li>
    </ul>
  </bef-pull-to-refresh>
  <p class="mt-2 px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Drag down from the top (touch or mouse).</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const refresher = document.querySelector('bef-pull-to-refresh');
  const list = refresher.querySelector('ul'); // a plain element inside a tag is part of its content, and changes show up
  refresher.props = {
    async onRefresh() {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));
      const li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'px-4 py-3 text-[13px]';
      li.textContent = `New update at ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' })}`;
      list.prepend(li);
    },
  };
</script>
```

## Punch card chart (punch-card-chart)

<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `${n} commits`,

</script>
```

## Radar chart (radar-chart)
Spider chart for several scores on the same axes: a pentagon with five, a hexagon with six. Shapes grow from the center on scroll; hover a legend name to highlight a series.

```

## Radial bar chart (radial-bar-chart)

  </div>
</bef-radial-bar-chart>
```

## Sankey chart (sankey-chart)

## Saved views (saved-views)
Menu of saved table setups: pick one, save the current one under a name, delete.
```html
<bef-saved-views :views='[{"id":"v1","name":"My open tasks"},{"id":"v2","name":"Overdue invoices"},{"id":"v3","name":"On leave this week"}]'></bef-saved-views>
<script type="module">
  const el = document.querySelector('bef-saved-views');
  let views = JSON.parse(el.getAttribute(':views'));
  let active;
  const draw = () => {
    el.setAttribute(':views', JSON.stringify(views));
    active ? el.setAttribute('active-id', active) : el.removeAttribute('active-id');
  };
  el.props = {
    onSelect(id) { active = id; draw(); },
    onSave(name) { const id = `v${Date.now()}`; views = [...views, { id, name }]; active = id; draw(); },
    onDelete(id) { views = views.filter((v) => v.id !== id); if (active === id) active = undefined; draw(); },
  };
</script>
```

## Schema form (schema-form)
Build a form from a description: text, email, number, date, select, textarea and checkbox fields, validation, and optional multi-step wizard with a stepper.
```html
<bef-schema-form submit-label="Create employee" id="g1"></bef-schema-form>
<script type="module">
const steps = [
    {
        title: 'Personal',
        description: 'Basic details for the employee record.',
        fields: [
            { name: 'first', label: 'First name', required: true, half: true },
            { name: 'last', label: 'Last name', required: true, half: true },
            { name: 'email', label: 'Work email', type: 'email', required: true, placeholder: 'name@company.com' },
            { name: 'phone', label: 'Phone', type: 'tel', half: true, validate: (v) => (v && !/^\d{10}$/.test(String(v)) ? 'Use 10 digits.' : undefined) },
            { name: 'dob', label: 'Date of birth', type: 'date', half: true },
        ],
    },
    {
        title: 'Job',
        description: 'Where they sit and what they do.',
        fields: [
            { name: 'dept', label: 'Department', type: 'select', required: true, half: true, options: [{ value: 'eng', label: 'Engineering' }, { value: 'design', label: 'Design' }, { value: 'sales', label: 'Sales' }] },
            { name: 'salary', label: 'Annual salary (₹)', type: 'number', half: true },
            { name: 'notes', label: 'Notes for HR', type: 'textarea', hint: 'Visible to the People team only.' },
        ],
    },
    { title: 'Confirm', fields: [{ name: 'ok', label: 'I confirm these details are correct', type: 'checkbox', required: true }] },
];

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  steps: [
    {
      title: "Personal",
      description: "Basic details for the employee record.",
      fields: [
        { name: "first", label: "First name", required: true, half: true },
        { name: "last", label: "Last name", required: true, half: true },
        {
          name: "email",
          label: "Work email",
          type: "email",
          required: true,
          placeholder: "name@company.com",
        },
        {
          name: "phone",
          label: "Phone",
          type: "tel",
          half: true,
          validate: (v) => v && !/^\d{10}$/.test(String(v)) ? "Use 10 digits." : void 0,
        },
        { name: "dob", label: "Date of birth", type: "date", half: true },
      ],
    },
    {
      title: "Job",
      description: "Where they sit and what they do.",
      fields: [
        {
          name: "dept",
          label: "Department",
          type: "select",
          required: true,
          half: true,
          options: [
            { value: "eng", label: "Engineering" },
            { value: "design", label: "Design" },
            { value: "sales", label: "Sales" },
          ],
        },
        { name: "salary", label: "Annual salary (₹)", type: "number", half: true },
        {
          name: "notes",
          label: "Notes for HR",
          type: "textarea",
          hint: "Visible to the People team only.",
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      title: "Confirm",
      fields: [
        {
          name: "ok",
          label: "I confirm these details are correct",
          type: "checkbox",
          required: true,
        },
      ],
    },
  ],
  onSubmit: async (v) => {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700));
    befui.toast.success("Employee created", `${v.first} ${v.last}`);
  },
};
</script>
```

## Select (select)

</div>
<script type="module">
  const dialog = document.querySelector('bef-shortcuts-dialog');
  document.querySelector('bef-button').addEventListener('click', () => dialog.setAttribute(':open', 'true'));
</script>
```

## Sidebar (sidebar)
Collapsible nav with groups, nested links and badges. Icon rail on desktop, slide-over on mobile.
```html
<bef-sidebar-provider>
  <div class="flex h-[440px] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl border bg-background text-left">
    <bef-sidebar>
      <bef-sidebar-header>
        <span class="grid size-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md bg-primary text-xs font-bold text-primary-foreground">A</span>
        <span class="truncate text-sm font-semibold">Acme HR</span>
      </bef-sidebar-header>
      <bef-sidebar-content>
        <bef-sidebar-group label="Overview">
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-layout-dashboard-icon></bef-layout-dashboard-icon>"' label="Dashboard" active></bef-sidebar-item>
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-inbox-icon></bef-inbox-icon>"' label="Inbox" badge="3" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
        </bef-sidebar-group>
        <bef-sidebar-group label="People">
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-users-icon></bef-users-icon>"' label="Employees" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-calendar-icon></bef-calendar-icon>"' label="Leave" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
        </bef-sidebar-group>
        <bef-sidebar-group label="Marketing">
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-megaphone-icon></bef-megaphone-icon>"' label="Campaigns" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
          <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-bar-chart-icon></bef-bar-chart-icon>"' label="Reports" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
        </bef-sidebar-group>
      </bef-sidebar-content>
      <bef-sidebar-footer>
        <bef-sidebar-item :icon='"<bef-settings-icon></bef-settings-icon>"' label="Settings" :active="false"></bef-sidebar-item>
      </bef-sidebar-footer>
    </bef-sidebar>
    <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <div class="flex h-14 items-center gap-3 border-b px-4">
        <bef-sidebar-trigger></bef-sidebar-trigger>
        <span class="text-sm font-medium">Dashboard</span>
        <bef-avatar name="Aarav Mehta" size="xs" class="ml-auto"></bef-avatar>
      </div>
      <p class="p-5 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Use the toggle to collapse the rail. Hover an icon to see its label. Below 1024px wide the sidebar becomes a slide-over.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</bef-sidebar-provider>
<script type="module">
  const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('bef-sidebar-item')];
  const title = document.querySelector('bef-sidebar-provider span.text-sm.font-medium'); // the page name next to the toggle
  let page = 'Dashboard', sub = 'Requests';
  const draw = () => {
    items.forEach((item) => {
      const label = item.getAttribute('label');
      item.setAttribute(':active', String(label === page));
      item.props = {
        onClick: () => { page = label; draw(); },
        ...(label === 'Leave' && {
          items: ['Requests', 'Balances', 'Calendar'].map((l) => ({ label: l, active: page === 'Leave' && sub === l, onClick: () => { page = 'Leave'; sub = l; draw(); } })),
        }),
      };
    });
    title.textContent = page === 'Leave' ? `Leave / ${sub}` : page;
  };
  draw();
</script>
```

## Slope chart (slope-chart)
One line per item between two moments. Steep means big change, crossing means swapped places. Green rose, red fell.
```html
<bef-slope-chart class="w-[34rem] max-w-full" from-label="2025" to-label="2026" :rows='[{"label":"Engineering","from":62,"to":78},{"label":"Sales","from":71,"to":69},{"label":"Support","from":55,"to":74},{"label":"Marketing","from":68,"to":81},{"label":"Finance","from":80,"to":77},{"label":"People","from":74,"to":74}]' id="g1"></bef-slope-chart>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `${n}%`,
};
</script>
```

## Sortable list (sortable-list)
Reorder rows by dragging, or with arrow keys on the grip. Controlled, no drag library.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-md gap-2">
  <bef-sortable-list :items='[{"id":"a","title":"Collect documents","owner":"Isha Nair","tag":"HR"},{"id":"b","title":"Create email and accounts","owner":"Rohan Das","tag":"IT"},{"id":"c","title":"Assign a buddy","owner":"Aarav Mehta","tag":"Team"},{"id":"d","title":"Schedule day-one orientation","owner":"Meera Iyer","tag":"HR"},{"id":"e","title":"Order laptop","owner":"Vikram Joshi","tag":"IT"}]' emit="change" id="g1"></bef-sortable-list>
  <p class="px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Drag a row, or Tab to a grip and press the up and down arrows.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
const { h: __h, T, Fragment: __Fragment } = befui;
const Badge = T.Badge;

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  renderItem: (s, i) => __h("div", { className: "flex items-center gap-2" }, __h("span", { className: "w-4 text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground" }, i + 1), __h("div", { className: "min-w-0 flex-1" }, __h("p", { className: "truncate font-medium" }, s.title), __h("p", { className: "text-xs text-muted-foreground" }, s.owner)), __h(T.Badge, null, s.tag)),
};
</script>
```
