-5","status":"up"},{"date":"Day -4","status":"up"},{"date":"Day -3","status":"up"},{"date":"Day -2","status":"up"},{"date":"Day -1","status":"up"},{"date":"Day 0","status":"degraded","note":"Delayed deliveries"}]}]' :incidents='[{"id":"i1","title":"Delayed webhook deliveries","state":"monitoring","updates":[{"time":"2 days ago, 10:40","text":"A fix is in place. We are watching the queue drain."},{"time":"2 days ago, 09:55","text":"Webhook deliveries are delayed by up to 20 minutes."}]},{"id":"i2","title":"Database failover","state":"resolved","updates":[{"time":"18 days ago, 14:20","text":"Resolved. All services are back to normal."},{"time":"18 days ago, 13:42","text":"The primary database failed over. API requests are failing."}]}]'></bef-status-page>

## Stepper (stepper)
Horizontal steps for multi-step forms and onboarding.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl gap-6">
  <bef-stepper :steps='[{"title":"Details","description":"Basics"},{"title":"Role","description":"Team &amp; level"},{"title":"Documents"},{"title":"Review"}]' :current="1"></bef-stepper>
  <div class="flex gap-2">
    <bef-button size="sm" variant="outline" :disabled="false">Back</bef-button>
    <bef-button size="sm" :disabled="false">Next</bef-button>
  </div>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const stepper = document.querySelector('bef-stepper');
  const [back, next] = document.querySelectorAll('bef-button');
  const count = JSON.parse(stepper.getAttribute(':steps')).length;
  const go = (delta) => {
    const current = Number(stepper.getAttribute(':current')) + delta;
    stepper.setAttribute(':current', String(current));
    back.setAttribute(':disabled', String(current === 0));
    next.setAttribute(':disabled', String(current === count));
  };
  back.addEventListener('click', () => go(-1));
  next.addEventListener('click', () => go(1));
  go(0);
</script>
```

## Stopwatch (stopwatch)
Start, pause, lap and reset, down to hundredths of a second.
```html
<bef-stopwatch></bef-stopwatch>
```

## Stream chart (stream-chart)
Streamgraph: smooth stacked flows around a center line to show shifts in mix over time. Ripples in from the left; hover a flow to focus it.
```html
<bef-stream-chart class="w-full max-w-xl" :labels='["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct"]' :series='[{"name":"Billing","data":[20,24,30,28,22,18,16,20,26,30]},{"name":"Login","data":[12,14,13,18,26,34,30,22,16,14]},{"name":"Bugs","data":[8,10,14,20,24,22,28,34,30,24]},{"name":"How-to","data":[14,12,11,10,12,14,16,18,20,22]}]'></bef-stream-chart>
```

## Streaming text (streaming-text)
Text that arrives word by word, each word fading up out of a soft blur, with a caret that follows the last one. Screen readers get the full text at once.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-lg gap-4">
  <p class="min-h-24 text-sm leading-7">
    <bef-streaming-text text="Carry-forward is capped at 12 days a year. Anything above that lapses on 31 March, and casual leave does not carry forward at all. If you are close to the cap, apply for leave before the financial year ends." :speed="16"></bef-streaming-text>
  </p>
  <bef-button variant="outline" size="sm" class="w-fit">Replay</bef-button>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const [text, button] = [document.querySelector('bef-streaming-text'), document.querySelector('bef-button')];
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const fresh = text.cloneNode(true);
    text.replaceWith(fresh); // a new element starts the stream again
    fresh.id = '';
    fresh.setAttribute('data-bef-fresh', '');
  });
</script>
```

## Sunburst chart (sunburst-chart)
A hierarchy as concentric rings: each ring splits its parent. Rings sweep outward; hover a slice to light up its parents and children.
```html
<bef-sunburst-chart :data='[{"label":"Engineering","children":[{"label":"Web","children":[{"label":"Frontend","value":2200000},{"label":"Backend","value":1800000}]},{"label":"Mobile","value":3000000},{"label":"Infra","value":2000000}]},{"label":"Sales","children":[{"label":"India","value":3000000},{"label":"Middle East","value":2000000}]},{"label":"People","children":[{"label":"Hiring","value":900000},{"label":"Benefits","value":600000}]},{"label":"Marketing","children":[{"label":"Paid","value":1500000},{"label":"Content","value":900000}]}]' id="g1"></bef-sunburst-chart>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `₹${(n / 1e5).toFixed(1)}L`,
};
</script>
```

## Swimlane timeline (swimlane-timeline)
Rows for people, teams or machines with bars on a shared day axis. Overlapping bars stack; a line marks today.
```html
<bef-swimlane-timeline class="w-[48rem] max-w-full" ~today="2026-10-14T00:00:00.000Z" :lanes='[{"name":"Aarav","items":[{"label":"Auth rewrite","start":"2026-10-01","end":"2026-10-09"},{"label":"Rate limits","start":"2026-10-12","end":"2026-10-20","tone":1}]},{"name":"Diya","items":[{"label":"Onboarding design","start":"2026-10-02","end":"2026-10-13","tone":2},{"label":"Design review","start":"2026-10-10","end":"2026-10-15","tone":3}]},{"name":"Kabir","items":[{"label":"Pricing page","start":"2026-10-05","end":"2026-10-11"},{"label":"Launch prep","start":"2026-10-14","end":"2026-10-24","tone":1}]},{"name":"Meera","items":[{"label":"Hiring loop","start":"2026-10-01","end":"2026-10-24","tone":3}]}]'></bef-swimlane-timeline>
```

## Swipe actions (swipe-actions)
List row you swipe left to reveal action buttons. Touch, mouse drag and keyboard.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-2">
  <bef-swipe-actions>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
      <bef-avatar name="Diya Rao"></bef-avatar>
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <p class="truncate text-[13px] font-medium">Offer letter for Kabir</p>
        <p class="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">Diya Rao · Sent it this morning, joining is 3 Nov.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </bef-swipe-actions>
  <bef-swipe-actions>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
      <bef-avatar name="Finance"></bef-avatar>
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <p class="truncate text-[13px] font-medium">September payroll is ready</p>
        <p class="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">Finance · Please review and approve before Friday.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </bef-swipe-actions>
  <bef-swipe-actions>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
      <bef-avatar name="Rohan Das"></bef-avatar>
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <p class="truncate text-[13px] font-medium">Laptop request</p>
        <p class="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">Rohan Das · Need a 16 GB machine for the new hire.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </bef-swipe-actions>
  <p class="px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Swipe or drag a row left. Keyboard: focus a row, press Left.</p>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const mails = ['Offer letter for Kabir', 'September payroll is ready', 'Laptop request'];
  document.querySelectorAll('bef-swipe-actions').forEach((row, i) => {
    row.props = {
      actions: [
        { label: 'Star', icon: '<bef-star-icon></bef-star-icon>', tone: 'warning', onClick: () => befui.toast.info('Starred', mails[i]) },
        { label: 'Archive', icon: '<bef-archive-icon></bef-archive-icon>', tone: 'primary', onClick: () => befui.toast.success('Archived', mails[i]) },
        { label: 'Delete', icon: '<bef-trash-icon></bef-trash-icon>', tone: 'danger', onClick: () => befui.toast.error('Deleted', mails[i]) },
      ],
    };
  });
</script>
```

## Switch (switch)

```html
<div class="grid gap-3">

  </div>

    <bef-switch id="s2"></bef-switch>
    <bef-label for="s2">Weekly digest</bef-label>
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
    <bef-switch id="s3" disabled></bef-switch>
    <bef-label for="s3">Disabled</bef-label>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Table of contents (table-of-contents)
On this page list that highlights the section currently in view.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-md gap-6 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_10rem]">
  <div class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
    <section id="toc-Overview" class="py-3">
      <h4 class="font-semibold text-foreground">Overview</h4>
      <p>Scroll the page and the list highlights the section in view.</p>
    </section>
    <section id="toc-Install" class="py-3">
      <h4 class="font-semibold text-foreground">Install</h4>
      <p>Scroll the page and the list highlights the section in view.</p>
    </section>
    <section id="toc-Usage" class="py-3">
      <h4 class="font-semibold text-foreground">Usage</h4>
      <p>Scroll the page and the list highlights the section in view.</p>
    </section>
    <section id="toc-Props" class="py-3">
      <h4 class="font-semibold text-foreground">Props</h4>
      <p>Scroll the page and the list highlights the section in view.</p>
    </section>
  </div>
  <bef-table-of-contents :items='[{"id":"toc-Overview","label":"Overview"},{"id":"toc-Install","label":"Install"},{"id":"toc-Usage","label":"Usage"},{"id":"toc-Props","label":"Props","level":2}]'></bef-table-of-contents>
</div>
```

## Tabs (tabs)
Underline or pill tabs on Radix Tabs.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-lg gap-8">
  <bef-tabs default-value="overview">
    <bef-tabs-list>
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="overview">Overview</bef-tabs-trigger>
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="leave">Leave</bef-tabs-trigger>
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="docs">Documents</bef-tabs-trigger>
    </bef-tabs-list>
    <bef-tabs-content value="overview" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Underline tabs, good for page sections.</bef-tabs-content>
    <bef-tabs-content value="leave" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Leave balance and history.</bef-tabs-content>
    <bef-tabs-content value="docs" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Contracts and ID proofs.</bef-tabs-content>
  </bef-tabs>
  <bef-tabs default-value="a">
    <bef-tabs-list variant="pill">
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="a">Weekly</bef-tabs-trigger>
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="b">Monthly</bef-tabs-trigger>
      <bef-tabs-trigger value="c">Yearly</bef-tabs-trigger>
    </bef-tabs-list>
    <bef-tabs-content value="a" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Pill tabs, good for toggling a view.</bef-tabs-content>
    <bef-tabs-content value="b" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Monthly view.</bef-tabs-content>
    <bef-tabs-content value="c" class="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Yearly view.</bef-tabs-content>
  </bef-tabs>
</div>
```

## Tag input (tag-input)
Type and press Enter to add chips. Backspace removes the last.
```html
<bef-tag-input class="w-full max-w-sm" :value='["Design","Remote"]' placeholder="Add skills, press Enter" emit="value-change"></bef-tag-input>
```

## Tag manager (tag-manager)
Create, rename, recolor and delete labels, with usage counts.
```html
<bef-tag-manager :tags='[{"id":"1","name":"Urgent","color":"rose"},{"id":"2","name":"Finance","color":"green"},{"id":"3","name":"Q4 planning","color":"violet"},{"id":"4","name":"Follow up","color":"amber"}]' :usage='{"1":12,"2":41,"3":7,"4":23}' emit="change"></bef-tag-manager>
```

## Team invite (team-invite)
Invite people by email: type or paste a list, each address becomes a chip, bad ones are flagged, one role for all, then Send.
```html
<bef-team-invite class="w-[34rem] max-w-full" :existing='["aarav@acme.co"]' id="g1"></bef-team-invite>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onSend: () => {
  },
};
</script>
```

## Terminal (terminal)
Dark terminal window with prompt lines and output. Optional typing animation.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-xl justify-items-center gap-3">
  <bef-terminal animate title="~/projects/hrms" :lines='["npx github:RandomKid24/befui init",{"text":"Wrote src/index.css and src/lib/utils.ts","kind":"muted"},"npx github:RandomKid24/befui add data-table kanban",{"text":"Installed 2 components and 1 dependency","kind":"output"},{"text":"Done in 2.1s","kind":"success"}]'></bef-terminal>
  <bef-button size="xs" variant="outline">Replay</bef-button>
</div>
<script type="module">
  // Replay: draw a fresh copy so the typing animation starts again
  const button = document.querySelector('bef-button');
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const old = document.querySelector('bef-terminal');
    old.replaceWith(old.cloneNode(true));
  });
</script>
```

## Testimonial card (testimonial-card)

```html
<bef-testimonial-card class="max-w-sm" :rating="5" name="Ananya Rao" role="Head of People, Northwind" quote="We replaced three spreadsheets with one screen. Approvals that took a day now take minutes."></bef-testimonial-card>
```

```html
<bef-theme-toggle></bef-theme-toggle>
```

```html
</div>
```

## Tilt card (tilt-card)
3D card that tilts toward the cursor with a moving sheen.
```html
<bef-tilt-card class="w-64 bg-primary text-primary-foreground">
  <p class="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-primary-foreground/70">Employee ID</p>
  <p class="mt-6 text-xl font-semibold">Aarav Mehta</p>
  <p class="text-sm text-primary-foreground/80">Engineering · EMP-0142</p>
</bef-tilt-card>
```

## Time picker (time-picker)
Hour, minute and AM/PM segments you type into or step with arrow keys. 12 or 24 hour.
```html
<div class="grid justify-items-start gap-4">
  <div class="grid gap-1.5">
    <span class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Shift starts (12-hour)</span>
    <bef-time-picker value="09:30" :minute-step="15" emit="value-change"></bef-time-picker>
  </div>
  <div class="grid gap-1.5">
    <span class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Shift ends (24-hour)</span>
    <bef-time-picker value="17:45" :hour12="false" :minute-step="15" emit="value-change"></bef-time-picker>
  </div>
  <p class="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Click a part, then use arrow keys or type. Value: 09:30 to 17:45</p>
</div>
```

## Timeline (timeline)
Vertical activity feed with tone-colored markers.
```html
<bef-timeline class="w-full max-w-sm" :items='[{"title":"Offer accepted","description":"Signed digitally by the candidate.","time":"2h ago","tone":"success","icon":"<bef-check-icon></bef-check-icon>"},{"title":"Documents uploaded","time":"Yesterday","tone":"info","icon":"<bef-file-icon></bef-file-icon>"},{"title":"Background check failed","description":"Address could not be verified.","time":"Mon","tone":"danger","icon":"<bef-x-icon></bef-x-icon>"},{"title":"Application received","time":"Last week","icon":"<bef-user-plus-icon></bef-user-plus-icon>"}]'></bef-timeline>
```

## Timer (timer)
Countdown with a ring that drains smoothly, presets and an onDone callback.
```html
<bef-timer id="g1"></bef-timer>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onDone: () => befui.toast.success("Time is up"),
};
</script>
```

## Toast (toast)
Call toast.success() from anywhere. No provider needed.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  <bef-button variant="outline" id="g1">Success</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" id="g2">Error</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" id="g3">Info</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" id="g4">With progress</bef-button>
  <bef-button variant="outline" id="g5">With undo</bef-button>
</div>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onClick: () => befui.toast.success("Saved", "Your changes are live."),
};
document.getElementById("g2").props = {
  onClick: () => befui.toast.error("Could not save", "Check your connection."),
};
document.getElementById("g3").props = {
  onClick: () => befui.toast.info("Heads up"),
};
document.getElementById("g4").props = {
  onClick: () => {
        const t = befui.toast.progress("Exporting report.pdf", "0%");
        let n = 0;
        const timer = setInterval(() => {
          n += 20;
          t.update(n, `${n}%`);
          if (n >= 100) {
            clearInterval(timer);
            t.success("Report ready", "report.pdf");
          }
        }, 500);
      },
};
document.getElementById("g5").props = {
  onClick: () => befui.toast.success("Message deleted", void 0, { action: { label: "Undo", onClick: () => befui.toast.info("Restored") } }),
};
</script>
```

## Toggle group (toggle-group)
A row of toggles where one or several can be pressed, for formatting toolbars.
```html
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-6">
  <bef-toggle-group type="multiple" variant="outline" size="icon" :value='["bold"]' aria-label="Text formatting" emit="value-change">
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="bold" aria-label="Bold">
      <bef-bold-icon></bef-bold-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="italic" aria-label="Italic">
      <bef-italic-icon></bef-italic-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="underline" aria-label="Underline">
      <bef-underline-icon></bef-underline-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
  </bef-toggle-group>
  <bef-toggle-group type="single" size="icon" value="left" aria-label="Alignment" emit="value-change">
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="left" aria-label="Align left">
      <bef-align-left-icon></bef-align-left-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="center" aria-label="Align center">
      <bef-align-center-icon></bef-align-center-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
    <bef-toggle-group-item value="right" aria-label="Align right">
      <bef-align-right-icon></bef-align-right-icon>
    </bef-toggle-group-item>
  </bef-toggle-group>
</div>
```

## Toggle (toggle)
Two-state button for favourite, bold or mute. Announces its state to screen readers.
```html
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
  <bef-toggle pressed size="icon" aria-label="Favourite" emit="pressed-change">
    <bef-heart-icon class="fill-current"></bef-heart-icon>
  </bef-toggle>
  <bef-toggle variant="outline" :pressed="false" size="icon" aria-label="Star" emit="pressed-change">
    <bef-star-icon></bef-star-icon>
  </bef-toggle>
  <bef-toggle variant="outline" :pressed="false" emit="pressed-change"><bef-bookmark-icon></bef-bookmark-icon> Save</bef-toggle>
</div>
```

## Token meter (token-meter)
How much of a model context window is used: a bar that turns amber and red as it fills, token counts, an optional cost, and a split by prompt, files and reply.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-6">
  <bef-token-meter :used="18400" :limit="200000" cost="$0.04"></bef-token-meter>
  <bef-token-meter :used="152000" :limit="200000"></bef-token-meter>
  <bef-token-meter :used="196500" :limit="200000"></bef-token-meter>
  <bef-token-meter :used="88000" :limit="200000" :segments='[{"label":"Prompt","tokens":12000},{"label":"Files","tokens":61000},{"label":"Reply","tokens":15000}]'></bef-token-meter>
</div>
```

## Tool call (tool-call)
A card for one tool or function call an AI made: name, running, done or failed, how long it took, and its input and output to expand.
```html
<div class="grid w-full max-w-lg gap-2">
  <bef-tool-call name="get_leave_balance" status="done" duration="0.4s" default-open input="{
  &quot;employee&quot;: &quot;aarav.mehta&quot;,
  &quot;type&quot;: &quot;casual&quot;
}" output="{
  &quot;balance&quot;: 7,
  &quot;carry_forward&quot;: 12
}"></bef-tool-call>
  <bef-tool-call name="search_policies" status="running" input="{ &quot;query&quot;: &quot;encashment&quot; }"></bef-tool-call>
  <bef-tool-call name="send_email" status="error" duration="1.2s" input="{ &quot;to&quot;: &quot;hr@acme.co&quot; }" output="SMTP 550: mailbox unavailable"></bef-tool-call>
</div>
```

## Tooltip (tooltip)
Hint on hover and focus. Optional title, keyboard shortcut, light style and arrow, plus an (i) InfoTip and a TruncatedText that only shows a tooltip when cut off.

```html
<bef-tooltip-provider>

    <div class="flex gap-2">
      <bef-tooltip content="Edit">
        <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Edit">

        </bef-button>
      </bef-tooltip>
      <bef-tooltip content="Share" side="bottom" arrow>
        <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Share">
          <bef-share2-icon></bef-share2-icon>
        </bef-button>
      </bef-tooltip>
      <bef-tooltip content="Notifications" side="right">
        <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Notifications">
          <bef-bell-icon></bef-bell-icon>
        </bef-button>
      </bef-tooltip>
      <bef-tooltip content="Save changes" :shortcut='["⌘","S"]'>
        <bef-button variant="outline" size="icon" aria-label="Save changes">
          <bef-save-icon></bef-save-icon>
        </bef-button>
      </bef-tooltip>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium">Net 30 terms<bef-info-tip title="Payment terms" content="Invoice is due 30 days after it is issued. Late fees start on day 31."></bef-info-tip></div>
    <div class="w-44 rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-sm">
      <bef-truncated-text>Quarterly vendor reconciliation report, final draft</bef-truncated-text>
    </div>
  </div>
</bef-tooltip-provider>
```

## Tour (tour)
Spotlight a part of the page and step through it with a card, for product walkthroughs.
```html
<div class="w-full max-w-md space-y-4">
  <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
    <div class="flex-1"><bef-input aria-label="Search" placeholder="Search"></bef-input></div>
    <bef-button variant="outline">New project</bef-button>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-lg border p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Ada commented on Roadmap · 2 minutes ago</div>
  <bef-button variant="soft">Take the tour</bef-button>
  <bef-tour :steps='[{"target":{"current":null},"title":"Search everything","body":"Find people, files and settings from one box."},{"target":{"current":null},"title":"Create something","body":"Start a project, invite a teammate or upload a file."},{"target":{"current":null},"title":"Stay in the loop","body":"Everything your team does shows up here.","side":"top"}]' :open="false" emit="open-change"></bef-tour>
</div>
<script type="module">
  // the tour points at elements by CSS selector
  const [search, create, activity] = [document.querySelector('bef-input').parentElement, document.querySelector('bef-button'), document.querySelector('.rounded-lg.border')];
  search.id = 'tour-search'; create.id = 'tour-create'; activity.id = 'tour-activity';
  const tour = document.querySelector('bef-tour');
  tour.setAttribute(':steps', JSON.stringify([
    { target: '#tour-search', title: 'Search everything', body: 'Find people, files and settings from one box.' },
    { target: '#tour-create', title: 'Create something', body: 'Start a project, invite a teammate or upload a file.' },
    { target: '#tour-activity', title: 'Stay in the loop', body: 'Everything your team does shows up here.', side: 'top' },
  ]));
  document.querySelectorAll('bef-button')[1].addEventListener('click', () => tour.setAttribute(':open', 'true'));
</script>
```

## Transfer list (transfer-list)
Two lists with arrows between them: tick items and move them across, each side with search and select all. For roles, columns or members.
```html
<bef-transfer-list class="w-[40rem] max-w-full" :items='[{"value":"viewer","label":"Viewer","disabled":false},{"value":"commenter","label":"Commenter","disabled":false},{"value":"editor","label":"Editor","disabled":false},{"value":"billing-admin","label":"Billing admin","disabled":false},{"value":"workspace-admin","label":"Workspace admin","disabled":true},{"value":"auditor","label":"Auditor","disabled":false},{"value":"support-agent","label":"Support agent","disabled":false},{"value":"developer","label":"Developer","disabled":false}]' :value='["viewer","editor"]' :titles='["Roles","Assigned"]' emit="change"></bef-transfer-list>
```

## Tree table (tree-table)
Table whose rows have child rows. Expand and collapse branches by click or arrow keys.
```html
<bef-tree-table class="w-full" :rows='[{"id":"eng","name":"Engineering","owner":"Aarav Mehta","budget":9000000,"spent":6400000,"children":[{"id":"eng-web","name":"Web platform","owner":"Ananya K.","budget":4000000,"spent":3100000,"children":[{"id":"eng-web-fe","name":"Frontend","owner":"Ananya K.","budget":2200000,"spent":1800000},{"id":"eng-web-be","name":"Backend","owner":"Kabir Shah","budget":1800000,"spent":1300000}]},{"id":"eng-mobile","name":"Mobile","owner":"Rohan Das","budget":3000000,"spent":2100000},{"id":"eng-infra","name":"Infrastructure","owner":"Kabir Shah","budget":2000000,"spent":1200000}]},{"id":"sales","name":"Sales","owner":"Vikram Joshi","budget":5000000,"spent":5300000,"children":[{"id":"sales-in","name":"India","owner":"Vikram Joshi","budget":3000000,"spent":3400000},{"id":"sales-me","name":"Middle East","owner":"Isha Nair","budget":2000000,"spent":1900000}]},{"id":"ops","name":"People Ops","owner":"Isha Nair","budget":1500000,"spent":900000}]' :default-expanded='["eng"]' id="g1"></bef-tree-table>
<script type="module">
const { h: __h, T, Fragment: __Fragment } = befui;
const Badge = T.Badge;

const lakh = (n) => `₹${(n / 100_000).toFixed(1)}L`;

document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  columns: [
    { key: "name", header: "Department" },
    { key: "owner", header: "Owner" },
    { key: "budget", header: "Budget", align: "end", render: (r) => lakh(r.budget) },
    { key: "spent", header: "Spent", align: "end", render: (r) => lakh(r.spent) },
    {
      key: "status",
      header: "Status",
      render: (r) => r.spent > r.budget ? __h(T.Badge, { variant: "danger" }, "Over") : __h(T.Badge, { variant: "success" }, "On track"),
    },
  ],
};
</script>
```

## Tree view (tree-view)
Nested expandable list with animated open and close.
```html
<bef-tree-view class="w-64 rounded-lg border p-2" :nodes='[{"id":"co","label":"Acme Inc","children":[{"id":"eng","label":"Engineering","children":[{"id":"a","label":"Aarav Mehta"},{"id":"b","label":"Isha Rao"}]},{"id":"des","label":"Design","children":[{"id":"c","label":"Kabir Shah"}]},{"id":"ceo","label":"Leadership"}]}]' selected="a" emit="select"></bef-tree-view>
<script type="module">
  const tree = document.querySelector('bef-tree-view');
  tree.addEventListener('bef:select', (e) => tree.setAttribute('selected', e.detail.id));
</script>
```

## Treemap (treemap)
Tiles sized by value, packed to stay square. Budget by team, revenue by product. Spring in on scroll; hover lifts one tile and dims the rest.
```html
<bef-treemap class="w-[36rem] max-w-full" :items='[{"label":"Engineering","value":9000000},{"label":"Sales","value":5300000,"color":"text-success"},{"label":"Marketing","value":3200000,"color":"text-warning"},{"label":"Operations","value":2600000,"color":"text-info"},{"label":"People","value":1500000},{"label":"Finance","value":1100000,"color":"text-success"},{"label":"Legal","value":600000,"color":"text-warning"},{"label":"IT","value":450000,"color":"text-info"}]' id="g1"></bef-treemap>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `₹${(n / 1e5).toFixed(1)}L`,
};
</script>
```

## Venn chart (venn-chart)
Two or three overlapping circles with a count in every region: who is in one group, who is in several.


## Version log (version-log)
Animated version history: the rail draws and dots pop as releases scroll in, entries slide in one by one, releases fold open, with a version jump strip and a kind filter.
```html
<bef-version-log class="w-full max-w-2xl" :releases='[{"version":"2.1.0","date":"2026-10-08","title":"Boards and reports","entries":[{"kind":"added","text":"Kanban board with keyboard moves.","extra":"<div class=\"flex gap-1.5\"><bef-badge variant=\"outline\">kanban</bef-badge><bef-badge variant=\"outline\">sortable-list</bef-badge></div>"},{"kind":"added","text":"Reports table block with a details drawer."},{"kind":"changed","text":"Data table rows can be clicked and have an actions menu."},{"kind":"fixed","text":"Combobox kept the old highlight after filtering."}]},{"version":"2.0.0","date":"2026-09-21","title":"New icon set","entries":[{"kind":"added","text":"94 hand-drawn icons with an optional draw animation."},{"kind":"changed","text":"Every component now uses the built-in icons."},{"kind":"removed","text":"The lucide-react dependency."}]},{"version":"1.4.0","date":"2026-09-02","title":"Time components","entries":[{"kind":"added","text":"Time picker, analog clock and world clock."},{"kind":"fixed","text":"Calendar skipped a day when the month changed."}]}]' :open-count="2"></bef-version-log>
```

## Virtual list (virtual-list)
Windowed list that renders only the visible rows. 100,000 items stay smooth.
```html
<div class="w-full max-w-lg">
  <p class="mb-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">100,000 rows, about 20 in the DOM at a time.</p>
  <bef-virtual-list row-height="44" aria-label="Employees" id="g1"></bef-virtual-list>
</div>
<script type="module">
const { h: __h } = befui;

// The rows are built here instead of being written out as JSON, so the page stays a few KB.
const depts = ["Design", "Sales", "Finance", "Ops"];
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  items: Array.from({ length: 100000 }, (_, i) => ({ id: i + 1, name: `Employee ${i + 1}`, dept: depts[i % 4] })),
  getKey: (r) => r.id,
  renderRow: (r) => __h("div", { className: "flex h-full items-center justify-between border-b px-4 text-sm" }, __h("span", null, __h("span", { className: "me-3 tabular-nums text-muted-foreground" }, "#", r.id), r.name), __h("span", { className: "text-muted-foreground" }, r.dept)),
};
</script>
```

## Voice wave (voice-wave)
Audio level bars for a voice input: they dance while listening and settle to a line when paused. Drive them with a real level or leave them idle.
```html
<div class="flex w-full max-w-md items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border bg-card p-3">
  <bef-button size="icon" variant="primary" shape="pill" aria-pressed aria-label="Stop listening">
    <bef-mic-icon></bef-mic-icon>
  </bef-button>
  <bef-voice-wave active class="flex-1"></bef-voice-wave>
  <span class="w-16 text-end text-xs text-muted-foreground">Listening</span>
</div>
<script type="module">
  const button = document.querySelector('bef-button');
  const wave = document.querySelector('bef-voice-wave');
  const label = button.parentElement.querySelector('span');
  let on = true;
  button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    on = !on;
    wave.setAttribute(':active', String(on));
    button.setAttribute('variant', on ? 'primary' : 'outline');
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
    label.textContent = on ? 'Listening' : 'Paused';
  });
</script>
```

## Waffle chart (waffle-chart)
A grid of 100 squares, each one percent of the whole. Squares pop in as a wave; hover a legend row to light up its squares.
```html
<bef-waffle-chart class="w-[34rem] max-w-full" :data='[{"label":"Done","value":46,"color":"text-success"},{"label":"In progress","value":27,"color":"text-primary"},{"label":"Blocked","value":9,"color":"text-warning"},{"label":"To do","value":18,"color":"text-muted-foreground/40"}]'></bef-waffle-chart>
```

## Waterfall chart (waterfall-chart)
How a start number becomes an end number: green adds, red subtracts, blue totals. Bars drop in one after another with connector lines.
```html
<bef-waterfall-chart class="w-[36rem] max-w-full" :steps='[{"label":"Revenue","value":120,"total":true},{"label":"COGS","value":-42},{"label":"Gross","value":78,"total":true},{"label":"Payroll","value":-31},{"label":"Marketing","value":-12},{"label":"Grants","value":6},{"label":"Tax","value":-9},{"label":"Profit","value":32,"total":true}]' id="g1"></bef-waterfall-chart>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  format: (n) => `₹${n}L`,
};
</script>
```

## Webhook log (webhook-log)
Recent webhook deliveries with status, time and duration. Open one for request and response bodies; failed ones can be resent.
```html
<bef-webhook-log class="w-[42rem] max-w-full" :deliveries='[{"id":"d1","event":"invoice.paid","status":200,"time":"2 min ago","duration":142,"url":"https://api.acme.co/hooks","request":"{\n  \"id\": \"evt_1\",\n  \"type\": \"invoice.paid\",\n  \"amount\": 4999\n}","response":"{ \"ok\": true }"},{"id":"d2","event":"customer.created","status":200,"time":"14 min ago","duration":98,"request":"{ \"id\": \"evt_2\", \"type\": \"customer.created\" }","response":"{ \"ok\": true }"},{"id":"d3","event":"payment.failed","status":500,"time":"1 hr ago","duration":1210,"attempt":3,"url":"https://api.acme.co/hooks","request":"{ \"id\": \"evt_3\", \"type\": \"payment.failed\" }","response":"Internal Server Error"},{"id":"d4","event":"subscription.updated","status":0,"time":"3 hr ago","attempt":5,"request":"{ \"id\": \"evt_4\", \"type\": \"subscription.updated\" }"}]' id="g1"></bef-webhook-log>
<script type="module">
document.getElementById("g1").props = {
  onResend: () => {
      },
};
</script>
```

## Week strip (week-strip)
Seven day pills with previous and next week arrows, a selected day and dots under days that have something. Arrow keys move the day.
```html
<div class="grid w-[26rem] max-w-full gap-3">
  <bef-week-strip ~value="now" :marks='{"2026-10-13":1,"2026-10-14":2,"2026-10-15":3,"2026-10-17":true}' emit="change"></bef-week-strip>
  <p class="text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">Selected: Wed Oct 14 2026</p>
</div>
```

## Word cloud (word-cloud)
Words sized by how often they appear, biggest in the middle. Words grow in; hover to focus one, or make them clickable filters.
```html
<bef-word-cloud class="w-[36rem] max-w-full" :words='[{"text":"pricing","value":98},{"text":"login","value":84},{"text":"export","value":61},{"text":"invoice","value":57},{"text":"slow","value":52},{"text":"mobile","value":48},{"text":"refund","value":40},{"text":"API","value":38},{"text":"dark mode","value":33},{"text":"SSO","value":30},{"text":"billing","value":28},{"text":"search","value":24},{"text":"import","value":22},{"text":"webhook","value":19},{"text":"GST","value":17},{"text":"crash","value":15},{"text":"reports","value":14},{"text":"onboarding","value":12},{"text":"widgets","value":10},{"text":"timezone","value":9}]'></bef-word-cloud>
```

## World clock (world-clock)
Cities with live local time, a day or night icon and the hour difference from you.
```html
<bef-world-clock class="w-80" :zones='[{"city":"Mumbai","timeZone":"Asia/Kolkata"},{"city":"London","timeZone":"Europe/London"},{"city":"San Francisco","timeZone":"America/Los_Angeles"},{"city":"Singapore","timeZone":"Asia/Singapore"},{"city":"Sydney","timeZone":"Australia/Sydney"}]'></bef-world-clock>
```
